"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export default function AdminSupport() {
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [isAuthenticated, setIsAuthenticated] = useState(false);
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [tickets, setTickets] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const hash = localStorage.getItem('admin_token_hash');
    if (hash) {
      try {
        const decoded = atob(hash);
        const rawPwd = decoded.split(':')[0] || '';
        verifyToken(rawPwd);
      } catch {
        setChecking(false);
      }
    } else {
      setChecking(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const hashToken = (pwd: string) => {
    const salt = Date.now().toString(36);
    return btoa(pwd + ':' + salt);
  };

  const verifyToken = async (token: string) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/verify-password', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ password: token }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        setIsAuthenticated(true);
        loadTickets();
      }
    } catch {} finally {
      setChecking(false);
    }
  };

  const handleLogin = async () => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/admin/verify-password', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ password }),
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      localStorage.setItem('admin_token_hash', hashToken(password));
      setIsAuthenticated(true);
      loadTickets();
    } else {
      alert('Invalid admin password');
    }
  };

  const loadTickets = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/tickets');
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        setTickets(data.tickets || []);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to load tickets', err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const updateStatus = async (ticketId: string, status: string) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/admin/tickets', {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ticketId, status }),
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        setTickets(prev => prev.map(t => t.id === ticketId ? { ...t, status } : t));
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to update ticket', err);
    }
  };

  if (checking) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#020203] text-white flex items-center justify-center">
        <div className="animate-spin w-8 h-8 border-2 border-yellow-400 border-t-transparent rounded-full"></div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#020203] text-white flex items-center justify-center">
        <div className="bg-[#07070a] border border-yellow-500/20 rounded-2xl p-8 max-w-md w-full mx-4">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-6 text-center text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-400 to-cyan-500">
            SUPPORT DASHBOARD
          </h1>
          <input
            type="password"
            value={password}
            onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleLogin()}
            placeholder="Admin Password..."
            className="w-full bg-white/5 border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-4 text-white placeholder:text-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-yellow-500 mb-4"
          />
          <button
            onClick={handleLogin}
            className="w-full py-3 rounded-xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-500 to-cyan-600 text-white hover:shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(234,179,8,0.4)] transition-all"
          >
            ACCESS SUPPORT
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#020203] text-white">
      <div className="bg-[#07070a] border-b border-white/5 px-6 py-4 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <h1 className="text-lg font-black tracking-tight">
            <span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-400 to-cyan-500">SUPPORT</span> TICKETS
          </h1>
          <div className="h-4 w-[1px] bg-white/10"></div>
          <a href="/admin" className="text-xs text-gray-500 hover:text-white font-bold uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors">Dashboard</a>
          <a href="/admin/hacker-tracker" className="text-xs text-gray-500 hover:text-white font-bold uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors">Hacker Tracker</a>
          <a href="/admin/support" className="text-xs text-yellow-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest">Support</a>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => {        localStorage.removeItem('admin_token_hash'); setIsAuthenticated(false); }}
          className="text-xs text-red-400 hover:text-red-300 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest"
        >
          Logout
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="p-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-mono">{tickets.length} total tickets</p>
          <button onClick={loadTickets} className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-400 border border-cyan-500/20 hover:bg-cyan-500/20">
            ↻ Refresh
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-[#07070a] border border-white/5 rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
          {loading ? (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-20">
              <div className="animate-spin w-8 h-8 border-2 border-yellow-400 border-t-transparent rounded-full"></div>
            </div>
          ) : tickets.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-16">
              <div className="text-4xl mb-4">🎉</div>
              <p className="text-gray-500 font-mono text-sm">No support tickets yet</p>
              <p className="text-gray-600 text-xs mt-2">Everything is running smoothly.</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-sm">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="text-gray-500 text-xs uppercase tracking-widest border-b border-white/5">
                    <th className="text-left py-3 px-4">ID</th>
                    <th className="text-left py-3 px-4">User</th>
                    <th className="text-left py-3 px-4">Subject</th>
                    <th className="text-left py-3 px-4">Status</th>
                    <th className="text-left py-3 px-4">Date</th>
                    <th className="text-left py-3 px-4">Actions</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {tickets.map((ticket: any) => (
                    <tr key={ticket.id} className="border-b border-white/5 hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors">
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono text-xs text-gray-400">#{ticket.id?.slice(0, 6)}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4">{ticket.user_email || 'anonymous'}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-gray-300 max-w-[200px] truncate">{ticket.subject || ticket.message?.slice(0, 40)}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        <span className={`text-[10px] font-mono font-bold uppercase px-2 py-0.5 rounded border ${
                          ticket.status === 'open' ? 'text-red-400 bg-red-500/10 border-red-500/30' :
                          ticket.status === 'in_progress' ? 'text-yellow-400 bg-yellow-500/10 border-yellow-500/30' :
                          ticket.status === 'resolved' ? 'text-green-400 bg-green-500/10 border-green-500/30' :
                          'text-gray-400 bg-gray-500/10 border-gray-500/30'
                        }`}>
                          {ticket.status || 'open'}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-gray-400 text-xs">{ticket.created_at?.slice(0, 10)}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        <select
                          value={ticket.status || 'open'}
                          onChange={(e) => updateStatus(ticket.id, e.target.value)}
                          className="bg-[#020203] border border-white/10 rounded-lg text-xs px-2 py-1 text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                        >
                          <option value="open">Open</option>
                          <option value="in_progress">In Progress</option>
                          <option value="resolved">Resolved</option>
                        </select>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: app/analyzer/page.tsx

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useUser } from '@clerk/nextjs';
import Navbar from '../components/Navbar';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
import UnifiedBackground from '../components/UnifiedBackground';
import GlitchText from '../components/GlitchText';
import ShareButton from '../components/ShareButton';
import CopyFullContent from '../components/CopyFullContent';
import RatingWidget from '../components/RatingWidget';
import { ErrorBoundary } from '../components/ErrorBoundary';

export default function SkillGapAnalyzer() {
  const { user } = useUser();
  const [role, setRole] = useState("");
  const [skills, setSkills] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [analysis, setAnalysis] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleAnalyze = async () => {
    setError(null);
    if (!role.trim() || !skills.trim()) {
      setError("SYSTEM ALERT: Please enter both Target Job Role and Current Skills!");
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setAnalysis("");

    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/analyzer', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ role, skills, userId: user?.id }),
      });

      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error);

      setAnalysis(data.analysis);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Matrix Overload");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <ErrorBoundary>
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#020203] text-white relative selection:bg-fuchsia-500/30 overflow-hidden font-sans pb-32 z-0 ">
      
      {/* Cyber Grid Background */}

          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-3 px-5 py-2 rounded-full bg-white/[0.04] border border-white/[0.08] text-xs font-bold tracking-widest mb-6 animate-shimmer-overlay shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(217,70,239,0.1)]">
            <span className="relative flex h-3 w-3">

              <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-3 w-3 bg-fuchsia-500 shadow-[0_0_10px_#d946ef]"></span>
            </span>
            <span className="text-gray-300 uppercase">Neural Diagnostics Active</span>
          </div>
          
          <h1 className="text-6xl md:text-8xl font-black tracking-tighter mb-4 uppercase drop-shadow-2xl">
            <GlitchText text="Skill Gap" tag="span" autoGlitch autoGlitchInterval={7000} />
            <br className="md:hidden" />
            <span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-400 via-fuchsia-400 to-pink-500">
              <GlitchText text="Analyzer." tag="span" autoGlitch autoGlitchInterval={5000} />
            </span>

        </div>

          <div className="mb-4 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/50 text-red-400 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-mono flex items-center gap-3 animate-pulse">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 bg-red-500 rounded-full shrink-0"></span>

          </div>
        )}



        {/* Input Interface */}

          {/* Main Terminal Box */}

            <div className="absolute -inset-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500/30 via-fuchsia-500/30 to-indigo-500/30 rounded-[2rem] opacity-40 group-hover:opacity-70 transition duration-700 animate-gradient-x"></div>
            
            <div className="h-full bg-[#07070a] rounded-[1.8rem] p-8 border border-white/10 relative shadow-2xl flex flex-col space-y-8">
              
              {/* Fake Terminal Header */}
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-white/10 pb-4">
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-red-500/80"></div>
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-yellow-500/80"></div>
                  <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-green-500/80"></div>
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs font-mono text-fuchsia-400/70 tracking-widest">sys.input_module</span>
              </div>

              <div className="flex-1 space-y-8">
                <div className="relative">
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-mono text-gray-400 mb-3 uppercase tracking-widest font-bold">
                    <span className="text-indigo-400">01 //</span> Target Job Role
                  </label>
                  <input 
                    type="text"
                    value={role}
                    onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="e.g. AI Engineer, Full Stack Developer..."
                    className="w-full bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-5 text-white text-lg font-medium focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 focus:bg-indigo-500/5 focus:shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(99,102,241,0.2)] transition-all placeholder:text-gray-600"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="relative flex-1 flex flex-col">
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-mono text-gray-400 mb-3 uppercase tracking-widest font-bold">
                    <span className="text-fuchsia-400">02 //</span> Current Skills / Resume
                  </label>
                  <textarea 
                    value={skills}
                    onChange={(e) => setSkills(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full flex-1 min-h-[220px] bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-5 text-gray-200 placeholder:text-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-fuchsia-500 focus:bg-fuchsia-500/5 focus:shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(217,70,239,0.2)] transition-all resize-none text-base leading-relaxed font-medium" 
                    placeholder="Paste your current tech stack, soft skills, or entire resume text here..."
                  ></textarea>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Action Trigger Box */}
          <div className="bg-[#07070a] border border-white/10 rounded-[1.8rem] p-8 shadow-2xl relative overflow-hidden flex flex-col justify-between">
            {/* Corner Decor */}
            <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-40 h-40 bg-indigo-500/10 animate-pulse-soft rounded-full"></div>

              <svg width="60" height="60" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1"><path d="M12 2L2 7l10 5 10-5-10-5zM2 17l10 5 10-5M2 12l10 5 10-5"/></svg>
            </div>

            <div>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-300 mb-4 uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="w-1.5 h-5 bg-gradient-to-b from-indigo-500 to-fuchsia-500 rounded-full shadow-[0_0_10px_#d946ef]"></span> Diagnostic Engine
              </h3>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 leading-relaxed font-medium">
                The neural engine will cross-examine your provided data against live global technical hiring metrics and highlight exactly what you are missing.
              </p>
            </div>
            
            <div className="mt-8">
              <button 
                onClick={handleAnalyze}
                disabled={loading}
                className={`group w-full h-20 rounded-2xl font-black text-xl transition-all duration-300 relative overflow-hidden
                  ${loading ? 'bg-[#111] text-indigo-400 border border-indigo-500/30' : 'bg-white text-black hover:scale-[1.02] shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(255,255,255,0.2)] hover:shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(217,70,239,0.4)]'}`}
              >
                {!loading && <span className="absolute inset-0 -translate-x-full group-hover:translate-x-full transition-transform duration-1000 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-white/30 to-transparent skew-x-12 pointer-events-none" />}

                    ANALYZING...
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="relative z-10 flex items-center justify-center gap-3">
                    Execute Audit
                    <svg className="w-6 h-6 group-hover:translate-x-2 transition-transform" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" strokeWidth="2.5"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M14 5l7 7m0 0l-7 7m7-7H3"></path></svg>
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Premium Extreme Output Section */}
        {analysis && (
          <div className="relative mt-16 group">
            <div className="absolute -inset-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600/40 via-fuchsia-500/40 to-indigo-600/40 rounded-3xl opacity-60 group-hover:opacity-90 transition duration-700 animate-gradient-x"></div>
            
            <div className="bg-[#050508] border border-fuchsia-500/30 rounded-3xl p-8 md:p-14 shadow-2xl relative">
              
              {/* HUD Header */}
              <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-6 mb-12 pb-8 border-b border-white/10 relative">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-5">
                  <div className="relative p-4 bg-indigo-500/10 rounded-2xl border border-indigo-500/40 shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(99,102,241,0.3)]">
                    <svg className="w-8 h-8 text-indigo-400 relative z-10" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M9 19v-6a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v6a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2zm0 0V9a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2v10m-6 0a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2m0 0V5a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2v14a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2z"></path></svg>
                    {/* Radar Ping */}
                    <div className="absolute inset-0 border-2 border-indigo-400 rounded-2xl animate-ping opacity-20"></div>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="text-3xl font-black text-white tracking-tight uppercase">Skill Matrix Breakdown</h2>
                    <p className="text-fuchsia-400 font-mono text-sm mt-1">Status: Decoded & Verified</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              
              {/* Custom Styled Markdown */}
              <div className="text-gray-300 font-medium">
                <ReactMarkdown
                  components={{
                    h1: ({node, ...props}) => <h1 className="text-4xl font-black text-white mb-8 mt-12 uppercase drop-shadow-md" {...props} />,
                    h2: ({node, ...props}) => <div className="mt-12 mb-6"><h2 className="inline-block text-2xl font-bold text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-400 to-fuchsia-400 border-b-2 border-fuchsia-500/30 pb-2" {...props} /></div>,
                    h3: ({node, ...props}) => <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-white mb-4 mt-8 flex items-center gap-2 before:content-['>'] before:text-fuchsia-500" {...props} />,
                    p: ({node, ...props}) => <p className="mb-6 text-gray-300 leading-relaxed text-lg" {...props} />,
                    ul: ({node, ...props}) => <ul className="mb-8 space-y-4" {...props} />,
                    li: ({node, ...props}) => (
                      <li className="flex items-start text-gray-300 bg-white/[0.02] p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/5 hover:border-indigo-500/30 hover:bg-indigo-500/5 transition-colors" {...props}>
                         <span className="text-indigo-400 mr-4 mt-0.5 font-bold text-xl leading-none">▹</span>
                         <span>{props.children}</span>
                      </li>
                    ),
                    strong: ({node, ...props}) => <strong className="text-white font-bold bg-fuchsia-500/10 border border-fuchsia-500/20 px-2 py-0.5 rounded shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(217,70,239,0.1)]" {...props} />,
                  }}
                >
                  {analysis}
                </ReactMarkdown>
              </div>
            </div>

n
            {/* Share, Copy & Rating */}
            <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-start md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-start md:items-center gap-3">
                <CopyFullContent text={analysis} />
                <ShareButton 
                  title={`Skill Gap Analysis for ${role}`}
                  text={analysis}
                />
              </div>
              <RatingWidget />
            </div>
          </div>

        )}

      </div>

    </div>
    </ErrorBoundary>
  );
}

FILE: app/api/admin/feedbacks/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabaseClient';
import { verifyAdminAuth, unauthorizedResponse } from '@/lib/adminAuth';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 30, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const authorized = await verifyAdminAuth(req);
    if (!authorized) return unauthorizedResponse();
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('feedbacks')
      .select('*')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(50);

    if (error) throw error;
    return NextResponse.json(data || []);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Feedbacks fetch error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json([]);
  }
}

================================================================================
FILE: app/api/admin/refunds/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabaseClient';
import nodemailer from 'nodemailer';
import { verifyAdminAuth, unauthorizedResponse } from '@/lib/adminAuth';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

// GET: Fetch all refund requests
export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 30, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const authorized = await verifyAdminAuth(req);
    if (!authorized) return unauthorizedResponse();
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('refund_requests')
      .select('*')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(100);

    if (error) throw error;
    return NextResponse.json({ refunds: data || [] });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Refunds fetch error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ refunds: [] });
  }
}

// PATCH: Approve or deny a refund request
export async function PATCH(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 30, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const authorized = await verifyAdminAuth(req);
    if (!authorized) return unauthorizedResponse();

    const { refundId, status, adminNote } = await req.json();

    if (!refundId || !status || !['approved', 'denied'].includes(status)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid request' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // Update the refund request
    const { data: refund, error: updateError } = await supabase
      .from('refund_requests')
      .update({ 
        status, 
        admin_note: adminNote || null,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      })
      .eq('id', refundId)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (updateError) throw updateError;

    // --- Send Email Notification ---
    const transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
      host: process.env.SMTP_HOST || 'smtp.gmail.com',
      port: parseInt(process.env.SMTP_PORT || '587'),
      secure: process.env.SMTP_SECURE === 'true',
      auth: {
        user: process.env.SMTP_USER,
        pass: process.env.SMTP_PASS,
      },
    });

    const isApproved = status === 'approved';

    const emailHtml = `
      <!DOCTYPE html>
      <html>
      <head><meta charset="utf-8"></head>
      <body style="font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif; background: #020203; padding: 40px 20px;">
        <div style="max-width: 520px; margin: 0 auto; background: #0a0a0f; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08); border-radius: 20px; padding: 40px;">
          <div style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 30px;">
            <div style="font-size: 48px; margin-bottom: 10px;">
              ${isApproved ? '✅' : 'ℹ️'}
            </div>
            <h1 style="color: #fff; font-size: 24px; font-weight: 800; margin: 0; letter-spacing: -0.5px;">
              Refund ${isApproved ? 'Approved' : 'Update'}
            </h1>
            <p style="color: #6b7280; font-size: 14px; margin-top: 8px;">EduAI Support</p>
          </div>

          <div style="background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06); border-radius: 12px; padding: 24px; margin-bottom: 24px;">
            <p style="color: #d1d5db; font-size: 15px; line-height: 1.7; margin: 0;">
              ${isApproved 
                ? 'Your refund request has been <strong style="color: #22d3ee;">approved</strong>. The amount will be credited to your original payment method within 5-7 business days.'
                : 'Your refund request has been <strong style="color: #f59e0b;">reviewed</strong>. Please read the admin note below for details.'
              }
            </p>
          </div>

          ${adminNote ? `
          <div style="background: rgba(251,191,36,0.05); border: 1px solid rgba(251,191,36,0.15); border-radius: 12px; padding: 20px; margin-bottom: 24px;">
            <p style="color: #fbbf24; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; margin: 0 0 8px 0;">Admin Note</p>
            <p style="color: #d1d5db; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.6; margin: 0;">${adminNote}</p>
          </div>
          ` : ''}

          <div style="text-align: center; padding-top: 24px; border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);">
            <p style="color: #6b7280; font-size: 12px; margin: 0;">© ${new Date().getFullYear()} EduAI. All rights reserved.</p>
            <p style="color: #4b5563; font-size: 11px; margin: 4px 0 0 0;">This is an automated notification. Please do not reply.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </body>
      </html>
    `;

    try {
      await transporter.sendMail({
        from: process.env.SMTP_FROM || '"EduAI Support" <noreply@eduai.com>',
        to: refund.user_email,
        subject: `Refund Request ${isApproved ? 'Approved ✅' : 'Update'} - EduAI`,
        html: emailHtml,
      });
    } catch (emailErr) {
      console.error('Email send failed (non-blocking):', emailErr);
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, refund });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Refund update error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to update refund' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/api/admin/stats/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabaseClient';
import { verifyAdminAuth, unauthorizedResponse } from '@/lib/adminAuth';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 30, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    // Verify admin auth
    const authorized = await verifyAdminAuth(req);
    if (!authorized) return unauthorizedResponse();
    // Fetch stats from Supabase tables
    const [usersRes, creditsRes, paymentsRes, ticketsRes] = await Promise.all([
      supabase.from('user_credits').select('*'),
      supabase.from('user_credits').select('credits'),
      supabase.from('payments').select('*'),
      supabase.from('support_tickets').select('*').eq('status', 'open'),
    ]);

    const users = usersRes.data || [];
    const credits = creditsRes.data || [];
    const payments = paymentsRes.data || [];
    const openTickets = ticketsRes.data || [];

    const totalUsers = users.length;
    const activeCredits = credits.reduce((sum: number, u: any) => sum + (u.credits || 0), 0);
    const totalRevenue = payments
      .filter((p: any) => p.status === 'completed')
      .reduce((sum: number, p: any) => sum + (p.amount || 0), 0);

    return NextResponse.json({
      totalUsers,
      activeCredits,
      totalRevenue,
      openTickets: openTickets.length,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Stats error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ totalUsers: 0, activeCredits: 0, totalRevenue: 0, openTickets: 0 });
  }
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/api/admin/tickets/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabaseClient';
import nodemailer from 'nodemailer';
import { verifyAdminAuth, unauthorizedResponse } from '@/lib/adminAuth';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 30, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const authorized = await verifyAdminAuth(req);
    if (!authorized) return unauthorizedResponse();
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('support_tickets')
      .select('*')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(50);

    if (error) throw error;
    return NextResponse.json({ tickets: data || [] });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Tickets fetch error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ tickets: [] });
  }
}

export async function PATCH(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 30, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const authorized = await verifyAdminAuth(req);
    if (!authorized) return unauthorizedResponse();

    const { ticketId, status, adminResponse } = await req.json();

    const updateData: any = { 
      status, 
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString() 
    };
    if (adminResponse) updateData.admin_response = adminResponse;

    const { data: ticket, error } = await supabase
      .from('support_tickets')
      .update(updateData)
      .eq('id', ticketId)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;

    // Send email notification if status changed and we have a response
    if (status && ticket?.user_email && (adminResponse || status === 'resolved')) {
      try {
        const transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
          host: process.env.SMTP_HOST || 'smtp.gmail.com',
          port: parseInt(process.env.SMTP_PORT || '587'),
          secure: process.env.SMTP_SECURE === 'true',
          auth: {
            user: process.env.SMTP_USER,
            pass: process.env.SMTP_PASS,
          },
        });

        await transporter.sendMail({
          from: process.env.SMTP_FROM || '"EduAI Support" <noreply@eduai.com>',
          to: ticket.user_email,
          subject: `Support Ticket #${ticket.tracking_id} - Status Updated`,
          html: `
            <!DOCTYPE html>
            <html>
            <head><meta charset="utf-8"></head>
            <body style="font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif; background: #020203; padding: 40px 20px;">
              <div style="max-width: 520px; margin: 0 auto; background: #0a0a0f; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08); border-radius: 20px; padding: 40px;">
                <div style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 30px;">
                  <div style="font-size: 48px; margin-bottom: 10px;">📬</div>
                  <h1 style="color: #fff; font-size: 24px; font-weight: 800; margin: 0;">Ticket Updated</h1>
                  <p style="color: #6b7280; font-size: 14px; margin-top: 8px;">
                    Ticket #${ticket.tracking_id}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div style="background: rgba(6,182,212,0.05); border: 1px solid rgba(6,182,212,0.15); border-radius: 12px; padding: 20px; margin-bottom: 20px;">
                  <p style="color: #22d3ee; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; margin: 0 0 8px 0;">Status: ${status?.toUpperCase()}</p>
                  ${adminResponse ? `<p style="color: #d1d5db; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.6; margin: 0;">${adminResponse}</p>` : ''}
                </div>
                <div style="text-align: center;">
                  <a href="${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || 'http://localhost:3000'}/support?track=${ticket.tracking_id}" 
                     style="display: inline-block; padding: 12px 24px; background: rgba(6,182,212,0.1); border: 1px solid rgba(6,182,212,0.2); border-radius: 10px; color: #22d3ee; text-decoration: none; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600;">
                    Track Your Request →
                  </a>
                </div>
                <div style="text-align: center; padding-top: 24px; margin-top: 24px; border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);">
                  <p style="color: #6b7280; font-size: 12px; margin: 0;">© ${new Date().getFullYear()} EduAI</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </body>
            </html>
          `,
        });
      } catch (emailErr) {
        console.error('Email send failed:', emailErr);
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Ticket update error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to update ticket' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FILE: app/api/admin/users/[id]/history/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabaseClient';
import { verifyAdminAuth, unauthorizedResponse } from '@/lib/adminAuth';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(
  req: Request,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 30, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const authorized = await verifyAdminAuth(req);
    if (!authorized) return unauthorizedResponse();

    const { id } = await params;

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('user_history')
      .select('*')
      .eq('user_id', id)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(50);

    if (error) throw error;

    return NextResponse.json({ history: data || [] });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Admin user history fetch error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ history: [] });
  }
}

FILE: app/api/admin/users/[id]/route.ts

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabaseClient';
import { verifyAdminAuth, unauthorizedResponse } from '@/lib/adminAuth';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(
  req: Request,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 30, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const authorized = await verifyAdminAuth(req);
    if (!authorized) return unauthorizedResponse();
    const { id } = await params;
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('user_credits')
      .select('*')
      .eq('user_id', id)
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;
    return NextResponse.json(data || {});
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('User fetch error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'User not found' }, { status: 404 });
  }
}

export async function PATCH(
  req: Request,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 30, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const authorized = await verifyAdminAuth(req);
    if (!authorized) return unauthorizedResponse();
    const { id } = await params;
    const body = await req.json();
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('user_credits')
      .update(body)
      .eq('user_id', id)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;
    return NextResponse.json(data);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('User update error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to update user' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 30, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    // Verify admin auth
    const authorized = await verifyAdminAuth(req);
    if (!authorized) return unauthorizedResponse();

      .from('user_credits')
      .select('*')
      .order('updated_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(50);

FILE: app/api/admin/verify-password/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 5, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const { password } = await req.json();
    const adminPasswordHash = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH;
    
    if (!adminPasswordHash) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Server misconfigured' }, { status: 500 });
    }
    
    const valid = await bcrypt.compare(password, adminPasswordHash);
    if (valid) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid password' }, { status: 401 });
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid request' }, { status: 400 });
  }
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/api/analyzer/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { auth } from '@clerk/nextjs/server';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { analyzeSchema } from '@/lib/validation';
import { checkAndDeductCredit, logUserHistory } from '@/lib/credits';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

// Helper to log usage history to Supabase


export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 10, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  let userId: string | null = null;
  let body: Record<string, unknown> | null = null;
  try {
    const { userId: authUserId } = await auth();
    body = await req.json() as Record<string, unknown> | null;
    userId = (body?.userId as string) || null;

    if (!body) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid request body' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    if (!body) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid request body' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const validation = analyzeSchema.safeParse(body);
    if (!validation.success) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: validation.error.flatten() }, { status: 400 });
    }
    const { role, skills } = validation.data;

    if (!authUserId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const creditCheck = await checkAndDeductCredit(authUserId);
    if (!creditCheck.ok) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: creditCheck.error || 'Credit limit exceeded' }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const apiKey = process.env.Gemini_Api_Key;
    if (!apiKey) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "API Key missing" }, { status: 500 });
    }

    const prompt = `You are an elite Tech Career Coach & Recruiter.
    Analyze the skill gap for a candidate targeting the role of "${role}".
    Their current skills/resume text: "${skills}".
    
    Provide a premium, highly detailed career roadmap in Markdown format. Include:
    1. Match Percentage (Give a prominent metric/percentage).
    2. Missing Core Skills (What they lack for this role).
    3. Missing Soft Skills/Tooling.
    4. Actionable 30-60-90 Day Learning Path to bridge this gap.
    Use professional markdown headers and clean bullet points.`;

    const geminiResponse = await fetch(
      `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.5-flash-lite:generateContent?key=${apiKey}`,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          contents: [{ parts: [{ text: prompt }] }]
        })
      }
    );

    if (!geminiResponse.ok) {
      const errData = await geminiResponse.json();
      throw new Error(errData.error?.message || "Gemini API failed");
    }

    const geminiData = await geminiResponse.json();
    const analysis = geminiData.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text;

    if (!analysis) throw new Error("No response from Gemini");

    return NextResponse.json({ analysis });

  } catch (error: unknown) {
    console.error("Analyzer Error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Failed to analyze skills" }, { status: 500 });
  } finally {
    if (userId && body) {
      logUserHistory(userId, 'analyzer', `role: ${String(body?.role || '').slice(0, 50)}, skills: ${String(body?.skills || '').slice(0, 50)}`);
    }
  }
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/api/checkout/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import Razorpay from 'razorpay';
import { auth } from '@clerk/nextjs/server';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

// Allowed plan configurations — server-side only, cannot be manipulated by client
const ALLOWED_PLANS = [
  { name: 'Starter Core', creditAmount: 150, price: 199 },
  { name: 'Scholar Protocol', creditAmount: 500, price: 499 },
  { name: 'Apex Overlord', creditAmount: 99999, price: 999 },
];

// Initialize Razorpay
const razorpay = new Razorpay({
  key_id: process.env.RAZORPAY_KEY_ID || '',
  key_secret: process.env.RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET || '',
});

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 10, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const { userId: authUserId } = await auth();
    if (!authUserId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const body = await req.json();
    const { userId, creditAmount, price } = body;

    if (userId !== authUserId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    // Server-side price validation — match against allowed plans
    const matchedPlan = ALLOWED_PLANS.find(
      p => p.price === price && p.creditAmount === creditAmount
    );

    if (!matchedPlan) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid plan selected. Price mismatch detected." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // Razorpay amount in paise (Rs 199 = 19900 paise)
    const options = {
      amount: matchedPlan.price * 100, 
      currency: "INR",
      receipt: `receipt_order_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000)}`,
    };

    // Create actual Razorpay order
    const order = await razorpay.orders.create(options);

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      orderId: order.id, 
      amount: options.amount, 
      currency: "INR",
      credits: matchedPlan.creditAmount,
      planName: matchedPlan.name,
    });

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Razorpay Error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Payment Order Failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FILE: app/api/feedback/route.ts

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabaseClient';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { feedbackSchema } from '@/lib/validation';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 20, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const result = feedbackSchema.safeParse(body);
    if (!result.success) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: result.error.flatten() }, { status: 400 });
    }
    const { userId, rating, message } = result.data;

    const { error } = await supabase.from('feedbacks').insert({
      user_id: userId || 'anonymous',
      rating: rating || 0,
      message: message || '',
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    });

    if (error) throw error;
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Feedback submission error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to submit feedback' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FILE: app/api/flashcards/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { auth } from '@clerk/nextjs/server';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { flashcardSchema } from '@/lib/validation';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

async function logUserHistory(userId: string, toolName: string, input: any, creditsUsed: number = 1) {
  try {
    const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || '';
    const supabaseKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || '';
    await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/rest/v1/user_history`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'apikey': supabaseKey,
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${supabaseKey}`,
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Prefer': 'return=minimal'
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        user_id: userId,
        tool_name: toolName,
        input: typeof input === 'string' ? input : JSON.stringify(input),
        credits_used: creditsUsed,
        created_at: new Date().toISOString()
      })
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('History log error:', e);
  }
}

async function checkAndDeductCredit(userId: string): Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }> {
  try {
    const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || '';
    const supabaseKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || '';
    const response = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/rest/v1/user_credits?user_id=eq.${userId}&select=credits`, {
      headers: { 'apikey': supabaseKey, 'Authorization': `Bearer ${supabaseKey}` }
    });
    const data = await response.json();
    const userRecord = data[0];
    if (!userRecord || userRecord.credits <= 0) {
      return { ok: false, error: "CREDIT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED: No credits left. Please upgrade." };
    }
    const currentCredits = userRecord.credits ?? 0;
    const newCredits = Math.max(0, currentCredits - 1);
    await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/rest/v1/user_credits?user_id=eq.${userId}`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: {
        'apikey': supabaseKey,
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${supabaseKey}`,
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Prefer': 'return=minimal'
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({ credits: newCredits, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
    });
    return { ok: true };
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Credit check error:', e);
    return { ok: true };
  }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 10, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  let userId: string | null = null;
  let body: { text?: string; userId?: string } | null = null;

  // Clone request so we can fall back to text() if JSON parse fails
  const clonedReq = req.clone();

  try {
    const { userId: authUserId } = await auth();
    body = await req.json();
    userId = body?.userId || null;
    const validation = flashcardSchema.safeParse(body);
    if (!validation.success) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: validation.error.flatten() }, { status: 400 });
    }
    const { text } = validation.data;

    if (!authUserId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const creditCheck = await checkAndDeductCredit(authUserId);
    if (!creditCheck.ok) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: creditCheck.error }, { status: 403 });
    }

    const apiKey = process.env.Gemini_Api_Key;
    if (!apiKey) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "API Key missing in environment variables" }, { status: 500 });
    }

    const prompt = `You are an AI tutor. Extract key concepts from this text and create flashcards. 
    If the text is just a greeting, a name, or very short, make a simple fun flashcard out of it.
    Return ONLY a valid JSON array containing objects. Each object must have exactly two keys: "front" and "back".
    Do NOT include markdown formatting like \`\`\`json.
    Text: ${text}`;

    const geminiResponse = await fetch(
      `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.5-flash-lite:generateContent?key=${apiKey}`,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          contents: [{ parts: [{ text: prompt }] }]
        })
      }
    );

    if (!geminiResponse.ok) {
      const errData = await geminiResponse.json();
      throw new Error(errData.error?.message || "Gemini API failed");
    }

    const geminiData = await geminiResponse.json();
    let rawText = geminiData.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text?.trim();

    if (!rawText) throw new Error("No response from Gemini");

    rawText = rawText.replace(/^```json/i, '').replace(/^```/i, '').replace(/```$/i, '').trim();

    let flashcards;
    try {
      flashcards = JSON.parse(rawText);
    } catch {
      // Fallback: split text into simple flashcards
      const lines = rawText.split('\n').filter((l: string) => l.trim());
      flashcards = lines.slice(0, 10).map((line: string, i: number) => ({
        front: line.replace(/^\d+[.):\s]+/, '').slice(0, 100) || `Concept ${i + 1}`,
        back: lines[i + 1]?.replace(/^\d+[.):\s]+/, '').slice(0, 200) || 'See notes',
      }));
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ flashcards });

  } catch (error: unknown) {
    console.error("Flashcard Error:", error);
    // Fallback: Generate flashcards from text splitting
    try {
      const text = await clonedReq.text();
      const cards = text.split('\n\n').map((block, i) => ({
        id: i,
        front: block.split('\n')[0] || `Card ${i+1}`,
        back: block.split('\n').slice(1).join('\n') || 'No content'
      }));
      return NextResponse.json({ flashcards: cards });
    } catch {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Failed to parse data" }, { status: 500 });
    }
  } finally {
    if (userId && body) {
      logUserHistory(userId, 'flashcards', `text: ${String(body?.text || '').slice(0, 100)}`);
    }
  }
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/api/generate/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { auth } from '@clerk/nextjs/server';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { generateSchema } from '@/lib/validation';
import { checkAndDeductCredit, logUserHistory } from '@/lib/credits';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';





export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 5, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  let userId: string | null = null;
  let syllabus: string | null = null;
  let days: string | null = null;
  try {
    const { userId: authUserId } = await auth();
    const body = await req.json();
    const validation = generateSchema.safeParse(body);
    if (!validation.success) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: validation.error.flatten() }, { status: 400 });
    }
    syllabus = validation.data.syllabus;
    days = validation.data.days || validation.data.targetDays || null;
    userId = validation.data.userId || null;

    if (!authUserId || (userId && userId !== authUserId)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    if (authUserId) {
      const creditCheck = await checkAndDeductCredit(authUserId);
      if (!creditCheck.ok) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: creditCheck.error || 'CREDIT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED' }, { status: 403 });
      }
    }

    const apiKey = process.env.Gemini_Api_Key;
    if (!apiKey) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "API key missing in .env.local" }, { status: 500 });
    }

    const prompt = `Act as an expert AI Curriculum Strategist. Break down this syllabus into a highly structured ${days}-day study plan. 
    You MUST return ONLY a valid JSON array of objects. DO NOT output any conversational text. Start directly with '[' and end with ']'.
    
    Each object inside the array must match this exact structure:
    {
      "day": 1,
      "title": "Short punchy day title",
      "description": "Brief summary of the day.",
      "timetable": [
        { "time": "09:00 AM - 11:00 AM", "slotTitle": "Concept Mapping", "details": "Core formulas and definitions." },
        { "time": "02:00 PM - 04:00 PM", "slotTitle": "Practice Drill", "details": "Solve numericals and key reactions." }
      ]
    }
    
    Syllabus data to structure: ${syllabus}`;

    const geminiResponse = await fetch(
      `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.5-flash-lite:generateContent?key=${apiKey}`,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          contents: [{ parts: [{ text: prompt }] }]
        })
      }
    );

    if (!geminiResponse.ok) {
      const errData = await geminiResponse.json();
      throw new Error(errData.error?.message || "Gemini API failed");
    }

    const geminiData = await geminiResponse.json();
    const planText = geminiData.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text;

    if (!planText) {
      throw new Error("No response from Gemini");
    }

    let cleanText = planText.trim();
    const firstBracket = cleanText.indexOf('[');
    const lastBracket = cleanText.lastIndexOf(']');
    
    if (firstBracket !== -1 && lastBracket !== -1) {
      cleanText = cleanText.substring(firstBracket, lastBracket + 1);
    } else {
      throw new Error("AI ne JSON array format nahi diya.");
    }

    const protocol = JSON.parse(cleanText);

    return NextResponse.json({ protocol });

  } catch (error: unknown) {
    console.error("Gemini API Error:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Failed to generate AI plan." }, { status: 500 });
  } finally {
    if (userId) {
      logUserHistory(userId, 'optimizer', `syllabus: ${String(syllabus || '').slice(0, 100)}, days: ${days}`);
    }
  }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { auth } from '@clerk/nextjs/server';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabaseClient';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET() {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 30, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const { userId } = await auth();
    if (!userId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ history: [] });
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('user_history')
      .select('*')
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(20);

    if (error) throw error;
    return NextResponse.json({ history: data || [] });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('History fetch error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ history: [] });
  }
}

FILE: app/api/intrusion-log/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabaseClient';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 10, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { ip_address, path, event_type, severity, user_agent } = body;

    if (!ip_address || !event_type) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Missing required fields' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const payload = {
      ip_address: ip_address,
      path: path || '/unknown',
      event_type: event_type,
      severity: severity || 'low',
      user_agent: user_agent || null,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    const { error } = await supabase.from('hacker_intrusions').insert([payload]);

    if (error) {
      console.error('Intrusion log insert error:', error);
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Intrusion log error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Internal error' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/api/newsletter/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { newsletterSchema } from '@/lib/validation';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 20, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const result = newsletterSchema.safeParse(body);
    if (!result.success) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: result.error.flatten() }, { status: 400 });
    }
    const { email } = result.data;

    // Log to Supabase newsletter table
    const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || '';
    const supabaseKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || '';

    const res = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/rest/v1/newsletter_subscribers`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'apikey': supabaseKey,
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${supabaseKey}`,
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Prefer': 'resolution=merge-duplicates'
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        email,
        subscribed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        source: 'blog',
      })
    });

    if (!res.ok && res.status !== 409) {
      const errData = await res.text();
      console.error('Newsletter subscription error:', errData);
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Subscribed successfully!" });
  } catch (error: unknown) {
    console.error('Newsletter API error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Failed to subscribe" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/api/podcast/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { auth } from '@clerk/nextjs/server';
import { checkAndDeductCredit, logUserHistory } from '@/lib/credits';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 10, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const { userId } = await auth();
    if (!userId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
    
    const { syllabus, days } = await req.json();
    
    const creditCheck = await checkAndDeductCredit(userId);
    if (!creditCheck.ok) return NextResponse.json({ error: creditCheck.error }, { status: 403 });
    
    const prompt = `Convert this ${days}-day study plan into an engaging podcast script. 
    Include: intro, daily breakdown with key concepts, study tips, and outro.
    Make it conversational and motivating. Syllabus: ${syllabus}`;
    
    const geminiResponse = await fetch(
      `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.5-flash-lite:generateContent?key=${process.env.Gemini_Api_Key}`,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ contents: [{ parts: [{ text: prompt }] }] })
      }
    );
    
    const data = await geminiResponse.json();
    const script = data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text;
    
    await logUserHistory(userId, 'podcast', `days: ${days}`, 1);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ script, audioUrl: null });
  } catch (error: any) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabaseClient';

import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 10, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const result = refundSchema.safeParse(body);
    if (!result.success) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: result.error.flatten() }, { status: 400 });
    }
    const { user_id, user_email, order_id, amount, reason } = result.data;

    const { data, error } = await supabase.from('refund_requests').insert({
      user_id: user_id || null,
      user_email,
      order_id: order_id || null,
      amount: amount || 0,
      reason,
      status: 'pending',
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    }).select().single();

    if (error) throw error;

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      success: true, 
      refund: data,
      message: 'Refund request submitted successfully. You will be notified via email.'
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Refund request error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to submit refund request' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FILE: app/api/support/route.ts

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabaseClient';
import { randomBytes } from 'crypto';
import nodemailer from 'nodemailer';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { supportSchema } from '@/lib/validation';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

function generateTrackingId(): string {
  return 'EDU-' + randomBytes(4).toString('hex').toUpperCase();
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 10, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const result = supportSchema.safeParse(body);
    if (!result.success) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: result.error.flatten() }, { status: 400 });
    }
    const { email, subject, message } = result.data;

    const trackingId = generateTrackingId();

    const { data, error } = await supabase.from('support_tickets').insert({
      user_email: email,
      subject: subject || 'No Subject',
      message,
      status: 'open',
      tracking_id: trackingId,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    }).select().single();

    if (error) {
      console.error('Support ticket insert error:', error);
      throw error;
    }

    // Send email with tracking ID
    try {
      const transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
        host: process.env.SMTP_HOST || 'smtp.gmail.com',
        port: parseInt(process.env.SMTP_PORT || '587'),
        secure: process.env.SMTP_SECURE === 'true',
        auth: {
          user: process.env.SMTP_USER,
          pass: process.env.SMTP_PASS,
        },
      });

      await transporter.sendMail({
        from: process.env.SMTP_FROM || '"EduAI Support" <noreply@eduai.com>',
        to: email,
        subject: `Support Ticket Created - #${trackingId}`,
        html: `
          <!DOCTYPE html>
          <html>
          <head><meta charset="utf-8"></head>
          <body style="font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif; background: #020203; padding: 40px 20px;">
            <div style="max-width: 520px; margin: 0 auto; background: #0a0a0f; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08); border-radius: 20px; padding: 40px;">
              <div style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 30px;">
                <div style="font-size: 48px; margin-bottom: 10px;">🎫</div>
                <h1 style="color: #fff; font-size: 24px; font-weight: 800; margin: 0; letter-spacing: -0.5px;">
                  Ticket Created!
                </h1>
                <p style="color: #6b7280; font-size: 14px; margin-top: 8px;">Your support request has been received.</p>
              </div>

              <div style="background: rgba(6,182,212,0.05); border: 1px solid rgba(6,182,212,0.15); border-radius: 12px; padding: 24px; text-align: center; margin-bottom: 24px;">
                <p style="color: #6b7280; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 700; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; margin: 0 0 8px 0;">Your Tracking ID</p>
                <div style="font-family: 'Courier New', monospace; font-size: 32px; font-weight: 900; color: #22d3ee; letter-spacing: 4px; background: rgba(6,182,212,0.08); border: 1px solid rgba(6,182,212,0.15); border-radius: 10px; padding: 16px; margin: 12px 0;">
                  ${trackingId}
                </div>
                <p style="color: #4b5563; font-size: 13px; margin: 12px 0 0 0;">
                  Use this ID to track your request status online.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div style="text-align: center;">
                <a href="${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || 'http://localhost:3000'}/support?track=${trackingId}" 
                   style="display: inline-block; padding: 14px 28px; background: linear-gradient(to right, #06b6d4, #6366f1); border-radius: 12px; color: #fff; text-decoration: none; font-size: 14px; font-weight: 700;">
                  Track Your Request →
                </a>
              </div>

              <div style="text-align: center; padding-top: 24px; margin-top: 24px; border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);">
                <p style="color: #6b7280; font-size: 12px; margin: 0;">© ${new Date().getFullYear()} EduAI</p>
                <p style="color: #4b5563; font-size: 11px; margin: 4px 0 0 0;">We typically respond within 24 hours.</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </body>
          </html>
        `,
      });
    } catch (emailErr) {
      console.error('Email send failed (non-blocking):', emailErr);
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      success: true, 
      ticket: data,
      tracking_id: trackingId,
      message: `Support ticket created! Your tracking ID is: ${trackingId}`
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Support ticket creation error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to create ticket' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/api/support/track/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabaseClient';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 30, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    const trackingId = searchParams.get('id');
    const email = searchParams.get('email');

    if (!trackingId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Tracking ID is required' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    let query = supabase
      .from('support_tickets')
      .select('id, tracking_id, subject, message, status, admin_response, created_at, updated_at, user_email')
      .eq('tracking_id', trackingId);

    // If email provided, also match for security
    if (email) {
      query = query.eq('user_email', email);
    }

    const { data, error } = await query.single();

    if (error || !data) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Ticket not found' }, { status: 404 });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ ticket: data });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Support track error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to fetch ticket' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FILE: app/api/user/credits/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { auth } from '@clerk/nextjs/server';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabaseClient';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET() {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 30, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const { userId } = await auth();
    if (!userId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ credits: 0, plan_name: 'Guest' });
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('user_credits')
      .select('credits, plan_name')
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .single();

    if (error || !data) {
      // Create initial record
      await supabase
        .from('user_credits')
        .insert({ user_id: userId, credits: 30, plan_name: 'Free Trial' });
      return NextResponse.json({ credits: 30, plan_name: 'Free Trial' });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ credits: data.credits || 0, plan_name: data.plan_name || 'Free Trial' });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Credits fetch error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ credits: 0, plan_name: 'Free Trial' });
  }
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/api/user/refunds/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabaseClient';
import { auth, clerkClient } from '@clerk/nextjs/server';
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rateLimit';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET() {
  const ip = (await headers()).get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!rateLimit(ip, 30, 60000)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rate limit exceeded' }, { status: 429 });
  }

  try {
    const { userId } = await auth();
    if (!userId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
    }

    // Get user email from Clerk SDK
    let email = '';
    try {
      const client = await clerkClient();
      const user = await client.users.getUser(userId);
      email = user.emailAddresses[0]?.emailAddress || '';
    } catch {
      return NextResponse.json({ refunds: [] });
    }
    if (!email) {
      return NextResponse.json({ refunds: [] });
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('refund_requests')
      .select('id, order_id, amount, reason, status, admin_note, created_at, updated_at')
      .eq('user_email', email)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .limit(20);

    if (error) throw error;
    return NextResponse.json({ refunds: data || [] });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('User refunds error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ refunds: [] });
  }
}
