================================================================================
FILE: app/api/webhook/clerk/route.ts
================================================================================

import { Webhook } from 'svix';
import { headers } from 'next/headers';
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const WEBHOOK_SECRET = process.env.CLERK_WEBHOOK_SECRET;

  if (!WEBHOOK_SECRET) {
    console.error('❌ CLERK_WEBHOOK_SECRET missing hai .env.local mein!');
    return new Response('Error', { status: 400 });
  }

  const headerPayload = await headers();
  const svix_id = headerPayload.get("svix-id");
  const svix_timestamp = headerPayload.get("svix-timestamp");
  const svix_signature = headerPayload.get("svix-signature");

  if (!svix_id || !svix_timestamp || !svix_signature) {
    console.error('❌ Clerk Headers missing hain!');
    return new Response('Error: Missing svix headers', { status: 400 });
  }

  // 🔥 FIX: req.json() ki jagah req.text() use karna zaroori hai signature ke liye
  const payload = await req.text();
  const wh = new Webhook(WEBHOOK_SECRET);
  let evt: any;

  try {
    evt = wh.verify(payload, {
      "svix-id": svix_id,
      "svix-timestamp": svix_timestamp,
      "svix-signature": svix_signature,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('❌ Signature Verification Failed:', err);
    return new Response('Error verifying webhook', { status: 400 });
  }

  // Agar yahan tak aaya, matlab Clerk ka message 100% sahi se receive ho gaya
  if (evt.type === 'user.created') {
    const { id } = evt.data;
    console.log(`🎉 NAYA USER AAYA HAI! ID: ${id}`);

    const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || '';
    const supabaseKey = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY || '';

    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/rest/v1/user_credits`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'apikey': supabaseKey,
          'Authorization': `Bearer ${supabaseKey}`,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          'Prefer': 'return=minimal'
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          user_id: id,
          credits: 50,
          plan_name: 'Free Trial'
        })
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        const errorText = await response.text();
        console.error("❌ SUPABASE REJECTED IT. Reason:", errorText);
      } else {
        console.log("✅ DATA SUPABASE MEIN CHALA GAYA!");
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("❌ Fetch fail ho gaya:", error);
    }
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ message: 'Webhook Processed' }, { status: 200 });
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/api/webhook/razorpay/route.ts
================================================================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import crypto from 'crypto';

const supabaseAdmin = createClient(
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!
);

// Webhook replay protection
const processedWebhooks = new Set<string>();

// Allowed plans for cross-checking
const ALLOWED_PLANS: Record<string, { credits: number }> = {
  'Starter Core': { credits: 150 },
  'Scholar Protocol': { credits: 500 },
  'Apex Overlord': { credits: 99999 },
};

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const body = await req.text();
    const webhookSecret = process.env.RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET;

    // Verify webhook signature
    if (webhookSecret) {
      const signature = req.headers.get('x-razorpay-signature');
      if (!signature) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Missing signature' }, { status: 400 });
      }

      const expectedSign = crypto
        .createHmac('sha256', webhookSecret)
        .update(body)
        .digest('hex');

      // Constant-time comparison to prevent timing attacks
      const trusted = crypto.timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(signature), Buffer.from(expectedSign));
      if (!trusted) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid signature' }, { status: 400 });
      }
    }

    const event = JSON.parse(body);

    // Idempotency check
    const eventId = event.payload?.payment?.entity?.id;
    if (eventId && processedWebhooks.has(eventId)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ message: 'Already processed' });
    }
    if (eventId) processedWebhooks.add(eventId);

    // Only process payment.captured events
    if (event.event !== 'payment.captured') {
      return NextResponse.json({ message: 'Ignored event type' });
    }

    const payment = event.payload.payment.entity;
    const orderId = payment.order_id;
    const paymentId = payment.id;
    const amount = payment.amount; // in paise
    const notes = payment.notes || {};

    // Validate against our allowed plans
    const planName = notes.plan_name || '';
    const plan = ALLOWED_PLANS[planName];
    if (!plan) {
      console.error(`Unknown plan: ${planName}`);
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unknown plan' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    // Verify amount matches expected plan price
    const expectedAmount = planName === 'Starter Core' ? 19900 :
      planName === 'Scholar Protocol' ? 49900 :
      planName === 'Apex Overlord' ? 99900 : 0;

    if (amount < expectedAmount) {
      console.error(`Amount mismatch for ${planName}: got ${amount}, expected ${expectedAmount}`);
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Amount mismatch' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const userId = notes.user_id;

    // Update or create payment record using admin client
    await supabaseAdmin.from('payments').upsert({
      order_id: orderId,
      payment_id: paymentId,
      user_id: userId,
      amount: amount / 100, // convert paise to rupees
      currency: payment.currency,
      status: 'captured',
      plan_name: planName,
      credits_purchased: plan.credits,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    }, { onConflict: 'order_id' });

    // Add credits to user using admin client
    if (userId) {
      const { data: existing } = await supabaseAdmin
        .from('user_credits')
        .select('credits')
        .eq('user_id', userId)
        .single();

      const currentCredits = existing?.credits || 0;
      const newCredits = currentCredits + plan.credits;

      await supabaseAdmin
        .from('user_credits')
        .upsert({
          user_id: userId,
          credits: newCredits,
          plan_name: planName === 'Apex Overlord' ? 'Apex Overlord' : planName,
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        }, { onConflict: 'user_id' });

      // Log the credit addition
      console.log(`✅ Added ${plan.credits} credits to user ${userId}. New balance: ${newCredits}`);
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ status: 'ok' });
  } catch (error: unknown) {
    console.error('Razorpay webhook error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Webhook error' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FILE: app/billing/page.tsx

"use client";
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import Navbar from '../components/Navbar';
import MagneticCursor from '../components/MagneticCursor';
import { useUser } from '@clerk/nextjs';
import UnifiedBackground from '../components/UnifiedBackground';
const loadRazorpayScript = () => {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const script = document.createElement('script');
    script.src = 'https://checkout.razorpay.com/v1/checkout.js';
    script.onload = () => resolve(true);
    script.onerror = () => resolve(false);
    document.body.appendChild(script);
  });
};

export default function Billing() {
  const { user, isSignedIn } = useUser();
  const [currentCredits, setCurrentCredits] = useState(50);
  const [currentPlan, setCurrentPlan] = useState('Free Trial');
  const [isRedirecting, setIsRedirecting] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Load actual user credits
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (isSignedIn) {
      fetch('/api/user/credits')
        .then(res => res.json())
        .then(data => {
          setCurrentCredits(data.credits || 0);
          setCurrentPlan(data.plan_name || 'Free Trial');
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    }
  }, [isSignedIn]);

  const handlePurchase = async (planName: string, creditAmount: number, price: number) => {
    setIsRedirecting(planName);
    
    setError(null);
    try {
      const sdkLoaded = await loadRazorpayScript();
      if (!sdkLoaded) {
        setError('Razorpay SDK failed to load. Please check your internet connection.');
        setIsRedirecting(null);
        return;
      }

      const response = await fetch('/api/checkout', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          userId: user?.id || 'guest',
          creditAmount: creditAmount,
          price: price 
        }),
      });
      
      const orderData = await response.json();

      if (!orderData.orderId) {
        setError("Server error. Razorpay order could not be created.");
        setIsRedirecting(null);
        return;
      }

      const options = {
        key: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RAZORPAY_KEY_ID, 
        amount: orderData.amount,
        currency: orderData.currency,
        name: 'EduAI Pro',
        description: `Upgrade to ${planName} (${creditAmount} Credits)`,
        order_id: orderData.orderId,
        theme: {
          color: '#06b6d4' 
        },
        handler: function (response: any) {
          window.location.href = `/billing/success?credits=${orderData.credits}`;
        },
        prefill: {
          name: user?.fullName || 'Premium User',
          email: user?.emailAddresses?.[0]?.emailAddress || 'user@eduai.com',
        }
      };

      const paymentObject = new (window as any).Razorpay(options);
      
      paymentObject.on('payment.failed', function (response: any) {
        setError("Payment Failed: " + response.error.description);
      });
      
      paymentObject.open();

    } catch (err) {
      setError("Billing system error. Please try again later.");
    } finally {
      setIsRedirecting(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#020203] text-white font-sans relative overflow-x-hidden selection:bg-cyan-500/30">
      
      <UnifiedBackground />

      <MagneticCursor />
      <Navbar />

      <div className="pt-36 pb-20 px-4 md:px-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto relative z-10 min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-center">
        
        {error && (
          <div className="mb-4 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/50 text-red-400 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-mono flex items-center gap-3 animate-pulse">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 bg-red-500 rounded-full shrink-0"></span>
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="text-center mb-16">
          <span className="text-xs font-mono text-cyan-400 tracking-[0.3em] uppercase border border-cyan-500/20 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-cyan-500/5">
            Billing & Core Quota
          </span>
          <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-6xl font-black tracking-tighter mt-6 mb-4 uppercase">
            Fuel Your <span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 via-indigo-400 to-purple-500">Arsenal.</span>
          </h1>
        </div>

        {/* Current Balance */}
        <div className="relative max-w-3xl mx-auto w-full rounded-3xl p-[1px] overflow-hidden mb-16 shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(0,0,0,0.8)]">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-500/20 via-purple-500/20 to-cyan-500/20 animate-pulse"></div>
          <div className="relative bg-[#07070a]/90 backdrop-blur-3xl rounded-3xl p-8 md:p-10 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-6 border border-white/5">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-mono text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">Current Active Account State</p>
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight">Plan Ecosystem: <span className="text-cyan-400">{currentPlan}</span></h2>
              <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-2 max-w-md font-light">Credits are consumed automatically every time the Curriculum Engine or AI modules deconstruct structural layouts.</p>
            </div>
            <div className="text-center md:text-right bg-white/[0.02] border border-white/5 px-8 py-6 rounded-2xl min-w-[200px] backdrop-blur-sm shadow-inner">
              <span className="text-5xl font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-br from-white to-gray-500 font-mono">{currentCredits}</span>
              <p className="text-[10px] font-mono text-cyan-400 uppercase tracking-widest mt-1">AI Engine Credits Remaining</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Pricing Cards */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-8 items-stretch w-full mt-6">

          {/* Tier 1 */}
          <div className="premium-pricing-card group">
            {/* The hidden layer for effects only */}
            <div className="absolute inset-0 rounded-[2rem] overflow-hidden z-0">
              <div className="card-bg-glow bg-cyan-500/10"></div>
              <div className="card-border-line"></div>
            </div>
            
            <div className="card-main-content">
              <span className="tier-tag text-cyan-400">01 // BASIC</span>
              <h3 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold text-white mt-2 mb-2 tracking-tight whitespace-normal break-words leading-tight">Starter Core</h3>
              <div className="flex items-baseline gap-1 mb-8 mt-2">
                <span className="text-4xl font-black font-mono">₹199</span>
                <span className="text-gray-500 text-xs">/ One Time</span>
              </div>
              <ul className="space-y-4 mb-10 text-sm text-gray-400 font-medium flex-grow">
                <li className="flex items-center gap-3"><span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 bg-cyan-400 rounded-full shadow-[0_0_8px_#06b6d4]"></span> 150 Engine Credits</li>
              </ul>
              <button 
                onClick={() => handlePurchase('Starter Core', 150, 199)}
                disabled={isRedirecting !== null}
                className="w-full py-3.5 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-widest bg-white/5 border border-white/10 hover:bg-white hover:text-black transition-all"
              >
                {isRedirecting === 'Starter Core' ? 'Loading...' : 'Deploy Starter Core'}

            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Tier 2 */}
          <div className="premium-pricing-card group relative highlighted-card">

            <div className="absolute -top-4 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 z-30 bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-500 to-indigo-500 text-black text-[10px] font-black tracking-[0.2em] px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full uppercase shadow-[0_0_25px_rgba(168,85,247,0.5)] whitespace-nowrap">
              Most Popular Core
            </div>

            {/* Background effects clipped to card shape */}
            <div className="absolute inset-0 rounded-[2rem] overflow-hidden z-0">
              <div className="card-bg-glow bg-purple-500/20 opacity-100"></div>
              <div className="card-border-line !bg-gradient-to-r !from-purple-500 !to-indigo-500 !opacity-40 group-hover:!opacity-100"></div>

          </p>
          <NewsletterSubscribe />
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: app/components/AmbientBackground.tsx

"use client";
import { Canvas, useFrame } from "@react-three/fiber";
import { useRef, useMemo } from "react";
import * as THREE from "three";

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   DNA DOUBLE HELIX — Chemistry meets Art
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function DNAHelix() {
  const groupRef = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
  const helixPoints = 40;
  const helixRadius = 2.5;
  const helixHeight = 18;

  const { strand1Geo, strand2Geo, rungGeos } = useMemo(() => {
    const s1Points: THREE.Vector3[] = [];
    const s2Points: THREE.Vector3[] = [];
    const rungs: { start: THREE.Vector3; end: THREE.Vector3 }[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < helixPoints; i++) {
      const t = (i / helixPoints) * Math.PI * 4;
      const y = (i / helixPoints) * helixHeight - helixHeight / 2;
      const x1 = Math.cos(t) * helixRadius;
      const z1 = Math.sin(t) * helixRadius;
      const x2 = Math.cos(t + Math.PI) * helixRadius;
      const z2 = Math.sin(t + Math.PI) * helixRadius;
      s1Points.push(new THREE.Vector3(x1, y, z1));
      s2Points.push(new THREE.Vector3(x2, y, z2));
      if (i % 4 === 0) {
        rungs.push({ start: new THREE.Vector3(x1, y, z1), end: new THREE.Vector3(x2, y, z2) });
      }
    }

    const curve1 = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(s1Points);
    const curve2 = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(s2Points);
    const strand1Geo = new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve1, 100, 0.06, 6, false);
    const strand2Geo = new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve2, 100, 0.06, 6, false);

    const rungGeos = rungs.map(r => {
      const dir = new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(r.end, r.start);
      const len = dir.length();
      const geo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.03, 0.03, len, 4);
      const mid = new THREE.Vector3().addVectors(r.start, r.end).multiplyScalar(0.5);
      return { geo, position: mid, length: len, direction: dir.normalize() };
    });

    return { strand1Geo, strand2Geo, rungGeos };
  }, []);

  useFrame((state) => {
    const t = state.clock.getElapsedTime();
    if (groupRef.current) {
      groupRef.current.rotation.y = t * 0.15;
      groupRef.current.position.y = Math.sin(t * 0.2) * 1.5;
    }
  });

  return (
    <group ref={groupRef} position={[8, 0, -12]}>
      <mesh geometry={strand1Geo}>
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#22d3ee" emissive="#22d3ee" emissiveIntensity={0.8} transparent opacity={0.15} />
      </mesh>
      <mesh geometry={strand2Geo}>
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#a855f7" emissive="#a855f7" emissiveIntensity={0.8} transparent opacity={0.15} />
      </mesh>
      {rungGeos.map((r, i) => (
        <mesh key={i} geometry={r.geo} position={r.position} quaternion={new THREE.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), r.direction)}>
          <meshStandardMaterial color="#34d399" emissive="#34d399" emissiveIntensity={0.5} transparent opacity={0.12} />
        </mesh>
      ))}
    </group>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   MOLECULAR STRUCTURE — Ball-and-Stick
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function MolecularStructure() {
  const groupRef = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);

  const { atoms, bonds } = useMemo(() => {
    const atomPositions = [
      [0, 0, 0], [2, 1, 0.5], [-1.5, 1.5, -0.5], [1, -1.5, 0.3],
      [-1, -1, 1.5], [2.5, -0.5, -1], [-2, 0, 1], [0.5, 2.5, -0.8],
    ];
    const colors = ['#22d3ee', '#a855f7', '#34d399', '#f472b6', '#fbbf24', '#22d3ee', '#818cf8', '#34d399'];
    const atoms = atomPositions.map((pos, i) => ({
      position: new THREE.Vector3(pos[0], pos[1], pos[2]),
      color: colors[i],
      radius: 0.18 + Math.random() * 0.12,
    }));
    const bondPairs = [[0,1],[0,2],[0,3],[0,4],[1,5],[2,6],[3,4],[1,7]];
    const bonds = bondPairs.map(([a, b]) => ({
      start: atoms[a].position,
      end: atoms[b].position,
    }));
    return { atoms, bonds };
  }, []);

  useFrame((state) => {
    const t = state.clock.getElapsedTime();
    if (groupRef.current) {
      groupRef.current.rotation.x = t * 0.08;
      groupRef.current.rotation.z = t * 0.05;
      groupRef.current.position.x = Math.sin(t * 0.15) * 2 - 8;
      groupRef.current.position.y = Math.cos(t * 0.12) * 1.5 + 3;
    }
  });

  return (
    <group ref={groupRef}>
      {atoms.map((atom, i) => (
        <mesh key={`atom-${i}`} position={atom.position}>
          <sphereGeometry args={[atom.radius, 12, 12]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial
            color={atom.color}
            emissive={atom.color}
            emissiveIntensity={1.2}
            transparent
            opacity={0.25}
          />
        </mesh>
      ))}
      {bonds.map((bond, i) => {
        const dir = new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(bond.end, bond.start);
        const len = dir.length();
        const mid = new THREE.Vector3().addVectors(bond.start, bond.end).multiplyScalar(0.5);
        return (
          <mesh key={`bond-${i}`} position={mid} quaternion={new THREE.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), dir.normalize())}>
            <cylinderGeometry args={[0.02, 0.02, len, 4]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color="#ffffff" emissive="#22d3ee" emissiveIntensity={0.4} transparent opacity={0.1} />
          </mesh>
        );
      })}
    </group>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   ORBITING ELECTRON SHELLS — Physics Visualization
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function OrbitalRings() {
  const groupRef = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
  const ring1Ref = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
  const ring2Ref = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
  const ring3Ref = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
  const electron1Ref = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
  const electron2Ref = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
  const electron3Ref = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);

  useFrame((state) => {
    const t = state.clock.getElapsedTime();
    if (groupRef.current) {
      groupRef.current.position.y = Math.sin(t * 0.1) * 0.5;
    }
    if (ring1Ref.current) ring1Ref.current.rotation.x = t * 0.3;
    if (ring2Ref.current) ring2Ref.current.rotation.z = t * 0.25;
    if (ring3Ref.current) ring3Ref.current.rotation.y = t * 0.2;

    // Electrons orbiting
    if (electron1Ref.current) {
      electron1Ref.current.position.x = Math.cos(t * 1.5) * 4;
      electron1Ref.current.position.y = Math.sin(t * 1.5) * 1.5;
      electron1Ref.current.position.z = Math.sin(t * 1.5) * 3;
    }
    if (electron2Ref.current) {
      electron2Ref.current.position.x = Math.cos(t * 1.2 + 2) * 3.5;
      electron2Ref.current.position.y = Math.sin(t * 1.2 + 2) * 3;
      electron2Ref.current.position.z = Math.cos(t * 1.2 + 2) * 2;
    }
    if (electron3Ref.current) {
      electron3Ref.current.position.x = Math.sin(t * 0.8 + 4) * 5;
      electron3Ref.current.position.z = Math.cos(t * 0.8 + 4) * 4;
      electron3Ref.current.position.y = Math.sin(t * 0.8 + 4) * 1;
    }
  });

  return (
    <group ref={groupRef} position={[0, 2, -8]}>
      {/* Orbital Rings */}
      <mesh ref={ring1Ref}>
        <torusGeometry args={[4, 0.015, 8, 80]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#22d3ee" emissive="#22d3ee" emissiveIntensity={2} transparent opacity={0.2} />
      </mesh>
      <mesh ref={ring2Ref}>
        <torusGeometry args={[3.5, 0.012, 8, 80]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#a855f7" emissive="#a855f7" emissiveIntensity={2} transparent opacity={0.15} />
      </mesh>
      <mesh ref={ring3Ref}>
        <torusGeometry args={[5, 0.01, 8, 80]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#34d399" emissive="#34d399" emissiveIntensity={1.5} transparent opacity={0.1} />
      </mesh>

      {/* Nucleus */}
      <mesh>
        <sphereGeometry args={[0.3, 16, 16]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#fbbf24" emissive="#fbbf24" emissiveIntensity={3} transparent opacity={0.4} />
      </mesh>

      {/* Electrons */}
      <mesh ref={electron1Ref}>
        <sphereGeometry args={[0.08, 12, 12]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#22d3ee" emissive="#22d3ee" emissiveIntensity={4} />
      </mesh>
      <mesh ref={electron2Ref}>
        <sphereGeometry args={[0.07, 12, 12]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#a855f7" emissive="#a855f7" emissiveIntensity={4} />
      </mesh>
      <mesh ref={electron3Ref}>
        <sphereGeometry args={[0.06, 12, 12]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial color="#34d399" emissive="#34d399" emissiveIntensity={4} />
      </mesh>
    </group>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   GRAVITY GRID FLOOR — Perspective Tron-like Grid
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function GravityGrid() {
  const gridRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
  const materialRef = useRef<THREE.ShaderMaterial>(null);

  const shader = useMemo(() => ({
    vertexShader: `
      varying vec2 vUv;
      varying float vDist;
      void main() {
        vUv = uv;
        vec4 worldPos = modelMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
        vDist = length(worldPos.xz);
        gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * worldPos;
      }
    `,
    fragmentShader: `
      varying vec2 vUv;
      varying float vDist;
      uniform float uTime;
      void main() {
        vec2 grid = abs(fract(vUv * 30.0 - 0.5) - 0.5) / fwidth(vUv * 30.0);
        float line = min(grid.x, grid.y);
        float gridAlpha = 1.0 - min(line, 1.0);
        float fade = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 20.0, vDist);
        float pulse = sin(uTime * 0.5 + vDist * 0.3) * 0.3 + 0.7;
        vec3 color = mix(vec3(0.133, 0.827, 0.933), vec3(0.659, 0.522, 0.984), sin(vUv.x * 3.14) * 0.5 + 0.5);
        gl_FragColor = vec4(color, gridAlpha * fade * 0.08 * pulse);
      }
    `,
    uniforms: {
      uTime: { value: 0 },
    },
    transparent: true,
    side: THREE.DoubleSide,
  }), []);

  useFrame((state) => {
    const t = state.clock.getElapsedTime();
    if (materialRef.current) {
      materialRef.current.uniforms.uTime.value = t;
    }
    if (gridRef.current) {
      gridRef.current.position.y = Math.sin(t * 0.3) * 0.5 - 6;
    }
  });

  return (
    <mesh ref={gridRef} rotation={[-Math.PI / 2.5, 0, 0]} position={[0, -6, -10]}>
      <planeGeometry args={[80, 80, 1, 1]} />
      <shaderMaterial ref={materialRef} args={[shader]} />
    </mesh>
  );
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   ENERGY WAVE — Sine Wave Visualizer
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function EnergyWave() {
  const lineObj = useMemo(() => {
    const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    const count = 200;
    const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3);
    geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
    const mat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: '#22d3ee', transparent: true, opacity: 0.06 });
    return new THREE.Line(geo, mat);
  }, []);

  useFrame((state) => {
    const t = state.clock.getElapsedTime();
    const positions = lineObj.geometry.attributes.position as THREE.BufferAttribute;
    const count = positions.count;
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const x = (i / count) * 40 - 20;
      const y = Math.sin(x * 0.3 + t * 2) * 0.8 + Math.sin(x * 0.7 + t * 1.5) * 0.4;
      positions.setXYZ(i, x, y, -15);
    }
    positions.needsUpdate = true;
  });

  return <primitive object={lineObj} />;
}

function GeometricMesh() {
  const meshRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
  const mesh2Ref = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
  const materialRef = useRef<THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>(null);

  const geometry = useMemo(() => new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(12, 1), []);
  const geometry2 = useMemo(() => new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(6, 0), []);

  useFrame((state) => {
    const t = state.clock.getElapsedTime();
    if (meshRef.current) {
      meshRef.current.rotation.x = t * 0.05;
      meshRef.current.rotation.y = t * 0.1;
      meshRef.current.position.y = Math.sin(t * 0.5) * 2;
    }
    if (mesh2Ref.current) {
      mesh2Ref.current.rotation.x = -t * 0.08;
      mesh2Ref.current.rotation.z = t * 0.06;
      mesh2Ref.current.position.y = Math.cos(t * 0.3) * 1.5;
      mesh2Ref.current.position.x = Math.sin(t * 0.2) * 3;
    }
    if (materialRef.current) {
      materialRef.current.emissiveIntensity = 0.2 + Math.sin(t * 2) * 0.15;
    }
  });

  return (
    <>
      <mesh ref={meshRef} geometry={geometry}>
        <meshStandardMaterial
          ref={materialRef}
          color="#050505"
          wireframe={true}
          emissive="#06b6d4"
          emissiveIntensity={0.2}
          transparent
          opacity={0.12}
        />
      </mesh>
      <mesh ref={mesh2Ref} geometry={geometry2}>
        <meshStandardMaterial
          color="#050505"
          wireframe={true}
          emissive="#a855f7"
          emissiveIntensity={0.15}
          transparent
          opacity={0.08}
        />
      </mesh>
    </>
  );
}

function EnergyOrbs() {
  const orb1Ref = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
  const orb2Ref = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);

  useFrame((state) => {
    const t = state.clock.getElapsedTime();
    if (orb1Ref.current) {
      orb1Ref.current.position.x = Math.sin(t * 0.4) * 8;
      orb1Ref.current.position.y = Math.cos(t * 0.3) * 4 + 2;
      orb1Ref.current.position.z = Math.sin(t * 0.2) * 3 - 5;
      orb1Ref.current.scale.setScalar(0.8 + Math.sin(t * 2) * 0.2);
    }
    if (orb2Ref.current) {
      orb2Ref.current.position.x = Math.cos(t * 0.3) * 6;
      orb2Ref.current.position.y = Math.sin(t * 0.5) * 3 - 1;
      orb2Ref.current.position.z = Math.cos(t * 0.15) * 4 - 5;
      orb2Ref.current.scale.setScalar(0.6 + Math.cos(t * 1.5) * 0.15);
    }
  });

  return (
    <>
      <mesh ref={orb1Ref}>
        <sphereGeometry args={[0.3, 16, 16]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial
          color="#22d3ee"
          emissive="#22d3ee"
          emissiveIntensity={2}
          transparent
          opacity={0.4}
        />
      </mesh>
      <mesh ref={orb2Ref}>
        <sphereGeometry args={[0.2, 16, 16]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial
          color="#e879f9"
          emissive="#e879f9"
          emissiveIntensity={2}
          transparent
          opacity={0.3}
        />
      </mesh>
    </>
  );
}

function ParticleField() {
  const pointsRef = useRef<THREE.Points>(null);
  const particleCount = 2500;

  const positions = useMemo(() => {
    const pos = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);
    for (let i = 0; i < particleCount; i++) {
      pos[i * 3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 80;
      pos[i * 3 + 1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 80;
      pos[i * 3 + 2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 80 - 10;
    }
    return pos;
  }, []);

  useFrame((state) => {
    if (pointsRef.current) {
      pointsRef.current.rotation.y = state.clock.getElapsedTime() * 0.02;
      pointsRef.current.rotation.x = Math.sin(state.clock.getElapsedTime() * 0.03) * 0.15;
    }
  });

  return (
    <points ref={pointsRef}>
      <bufferGeometry>
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-position"
          args={[positions, 3]}
        />
      </bufferGeometry>
      <pointsMaterial size={0.025} color="#a855f7" transparent opacity={0.35} sizeAttenuation />
    </points>
  );
}

export default function AmbientBackground() {
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 pointer-events-none z-0" style={{ background: '#010101' }}>
      <Canvas camera={{ position: [0, 0, 25], fov: 45 }}>
        <ambientLight intensity={0.15} />
        <pointLight position={[10, 10, 10]} intensity={2.5} color="#22d3ee" />
        <pointLight position={[-10, -10, -10]} intensity={2.5} color="#e879f9" />
        <pointLight position={[0, 5, -5]} intensity={1} color="#06b6d4" />
        <GeometricMesh />
        <EnergyOrbs />
        <ParticleField />
        <DNAHelix />
        <MolecularStructure />
        <OrbitalRings />
        <GravityGrid />
        <EnergyWave />
      </Canvas>
      {/* Vignette overlay for depth */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[radial-gradient(circle_at_center,transparent_0%,#010101_100%)] opacity-85" />
      
      {/* CRT Scan line overlay */}
      <div 
        className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none opacity-15"
        style={{
          backgroundImage: 'linear-gradient(rgba(255, 255, 255, 0) 50%, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25) 50%), linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255, 0, 0, 0.06), rgba(0, 255, 0, 0.02), rgba(0, 0, 255, 0.06))',
          backgroundSize: '100% 4px, 6px 100%'
        }}
      />

      {/* Subtle horizontal scan line */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none overflow-hidden">
        <div 
          className="absolute w-full h-[1px] bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-cyan-500/20 to-transparent"
          style={{ animation: 'scan-line 8s linear infinite' }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: app/components/CopyFullContent.tsx

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react';

interface CopyFullContentProps {
  text: string;
  label?: string;
  className?: string;
}

export default function CopyFullContent({ text, label = "Copy Full Content", className = "" }: CopyFullContentProps) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const handleCopy = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2500);
    } catch {
      const textarea = document.createElement('textarea');
      textarea.value = text;
      document.body.appendChild(textarea);
      textarea.select();
      document.execCommand('copy');
      document.body.removeChild(textarea);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2500);
    }
  };

  return (
    <button
      onClick={handleCopy}
      className={`relative flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm transition-all duration-300 active:scale-95 ${
        copied
          ? 'bg-green-500/20 text-green-400 border border-green-500/30 shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(34,197,94,0.15)]'
          : 'bg-white/[0.06] text-white/80 border border-white/[0.10] hover:bg-white/[0.10] hover:text-white hover:border-cyan-500/40 hover:shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(6,182,212,0.1)]'
      } ${className}`}
    >
      {copied ? (
        <>
          <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" strokeWidth="2.5">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7" />
          </svg>
          Copied to Clipboard!
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" strokeWidth="2">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M8 5H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2h10a2 2 0 002-2v-1M8 5a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2M8 5a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2m0 0h2a2 2 0 012 2v3m2 4H10m0 0l3-3m-3 3l3 3" />
          </svg>
          {label}
        </>
      )}
    </button>
  );
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx
================================================================================

'use client';
import { Component, ReactNode } from 'react';

interface Props { children: ReactNode; fallback?: ReactNode; }
interface State { hasError: boolean; error?: Error; }

export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<Props, State> {
  constructor(props: Props) { super(props); this.state = { hasError: false }; }
  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): State { return { hasError: true, error }; }
  componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: React.ErrorInfo) { console.error('ErrorBoundary caught:', error, info); }
  render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return this.props.fallback || (
        <div className="p-8 text-center">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-red-400 mb-2">Something went wrong</h2>
          <button onClick={() => this.setState({ hasError: false })} className="px-4 py-2 bg-cyan-500 rounded-lg">Try again</button>
        </div>
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}

            >
              {/* Subtle top glare fade on hover */}
              <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-1/2 bg-white/5 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-300 -z-10"></div>
              
              {/* Glowing Top Line */}
              <div className={`h-1 w-12 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r ${tool.color} mb-6 group-hover:w-full transition-all duration-300`}></div>
              
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-white mb-3 tracking-tight">{tool.title}</h3>
              <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm leading-relaxed mb-6">
                {tool.description}
              </p>
              
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-white flex items-center gap-2 group-hover:gap-3 transition-all">
                Try Now <span className="text-gray-500 group-hover:text-white transition-colors">→</span>
              </span>
            </div>
          </Link>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/components/GlitchText.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";
import { useState, useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useMemo } from "react";

function usePrefersReducedMotion() {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const mq = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
    setReduced(mq.matches);
    const handler = (e: MediaQueryListEvent) => setReduced(e.matches);
    mq.addEventListener('change', handler);
    return () => mq.removeEventListener('change', handler);
  }, []);
  return reduced;
}

const SCRAMBLE = "!@#$%^&*()_+-=[]01";

interface GlitchTextProps {
  text: string;
  className?: string;
  tag?: "h1" | "h2" | "h3" | "span" | "p";
  glitchOnHover?: boolean;
  autoGlitch?: boolean;
  autoGlitchInterval?: number;
  rgbSplit?: boolean;
}

export default function GlitchText({
  text,
  className = "",
  tag: Tag = "span",
  glitchOnHover = true,
  autoGlitch = false,
  autoGlitchInterval = 5000,
  rgbSplit = true,
}: GlitchTextProps) {
  const [displayText, setDisplayText] = useState(text);
  const [isGlitching, setIsGlitching] = useState(false);
  const [intensity, setIntensity] = useState(0);
  const intervalRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => { setDisplayText(text); }, [text]);

  const triggerGlitch = useCallback(() => {
    if (isGlitching) return;
    setIsGlitching(true);
    let i = 0;
    const max = 12;
    const iv = setInterval(() => {
      setDisplayText(
        text.split("").map((ch, idx) => {
          if (idx < (i * text.length) / max) return text[idx];
          return ch === " " ? " " : SCRAMBLE[Math.floor(Math.random() * SCRAMBLE.length)];
        }).join("")
      );
      setIntensity(Math.sin((i / max) * Math.PI));
      i++;
      if (i > max) {
        clearInterval(iv);
        setDisplayText(text);
        setIntensity(0);
        setIsGlitching(false);
      }
    }, 40);
  }, [text, isGlitching]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (autoGlitch) {
      intervalRef.current = setInterval(triggerGlitch, autoGlitchInterval);
      return () => { if (intervalRef.current) clearInterval(intervalRef.current); };
    }
  }, [autoGlitch, autoGlitchInterval, triggerGlitch]);

  useEffect(() => () => { if (intervalRef.current) clearInterval(intervalRef.current); }, []);

  const prefersReduced = usePrefersReducedMotion();

  const clipA = useMemo(() => "inset(" + (30 + Math.random() * 20) + "% 0 " + (20 + Math.random() * 20) + "% 0)", [intensity]);
  const clipB = useMemo(() => "inset(" + (10 + Math.random() * 30) + "% 0 " + (30 + Math.random() * 30) + "% 0)", [intensity]);
  const clipC = useMemo(() => "inset(" + (Math.random() * 50) + "% 0 " + (Math.random() * 50) + "% 0)", [intensity]);

  if (prefersReduced) {
    return <Tag className={"relative inline-block " + className}><span className="relative z-10">{text}</span></Tag>;
  }

  return (
    <Tag
      className={"relative inline-block " + className}
      onMouseEnter={glitchOnHover ? triggerGlitch : undefined}
      style={{ cursor: "default" }}
    >
      <span className="relative z-10">{displayText}</span>

      {rgbSplit && intensity > 0 && (
        <>
          <span
            className="absolute inset-0 z-20 pointer-events-none"
            style={{
              color: "rgba(255, 0, 0, 0.5)",
              transform: "translate(" + intensity * 3 + "px, " + intensity * -1 + "px)",
              clipPath: clipA,
              mixBlendMode: "screen" as const,
            }}
          >
            {displayText}
          </span>
          <span
            className="absolute inset-0 z-20 pointer-events-none"
            style={{
              color: "rgba(0, 100, 255, 0.5)",
              transform: "translate(" + intensity * -3 + "px, " + intensity + "px)",
              clipPath: clipB,
              mixBlendMode: "screen" as const,
            }}
          >
            {displayText}
          </span>
          <span
            className="absolute inset-0 z-20 pointer-events-none"
            style={{
              color: "rgba(0, 255, 100, 0.3)",
              transform: "translate(" + intensity + "px, " + intensity * 2 + "px)",
              clipPath: clipC,
              mixBlendMode: "screen" as const,
            }}
          >
            {displayText}
          </span>
        </>
      )}

      {intensity > 0 && (
        <div
          className="absolute left-0 right-0 h-[2px] bg-white/10 pointer-events-none z-30"
          style={{
            top: (30 + Math.random() * 40) + "%",
            transform: "translateX(" + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 20 + "px)",
            opacity: intensity,
          }}
        />
      )}
    </Tag>
  );
}

FILE: app/components/HighTierBackground.tsx

"use client";
import { Canvas, useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import * as THREE from 'three';

function MorphingIcosahedron() {
  const meshRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
  const geometry = useMemo(() => new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(2, 2), []);
  
  useFrame((state) => {
    if (!meshRef.current) return;
    const t = state.clock.getElapsedTime();
    meshRef.current.rotation.x = t * 0.1;
    meshRef.current.rotation.y = t * 0.15;
    const positions = meshRef.current.geometry.attributes.position as THREE.BufferAttribute;
    for (let i = 0; i < positions.count; i++) {
      const x = positions.getX(i);
      const y = positions.getY(i);
      const z = positions.getZ(i);
      const noise = Math.sin(x * 2 + t) * Math.cos(y * 2 + t) * 0.1;
      positions.setXYZ(i, x + noise, y + noise, z + noise);
    }
    positions.needsUpdate = true;
  });

  return (
    <mesh ref={meshRef} geometry={geometry}>
      <meshStandardMaterial
        color="#050505"
        wireframe
        emissive="#22d3ee"
        emissiveIntensity={0.15}
        transparent
        opacity={0.3}
      />
    </mesh>
  );
}

export default function HighTierBackground() {
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 pointer-events-none z-0">
      <Canvas camera={{ position: [0, 0, 8], fov: 45 }} dpr={[1, 1.5]}>
        <ambientLight intensity={0.2} />
        <pointLight position={[10, 10, 10]} intensity={1} color="#22d3ee" />
        <pointLight position={[-10, -10, -10]} intensity={0.5} color="#a855f7" />
        <MorphingIcosahedron />
      </Canvas>
      <div className="absolute inset-0" style={{ background: 'radial-gradient(ellipse at center, transparent 30%, #020203 100%)' }} />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: app/components/HolographicLoader.tsx

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";

const BOOT_PHASES = [
  { label: "INITIALIZING QUANTUM CORE", icon: "\u25C6" },
  { label: "LOADING NEURAL SUBSTRATE", icon: "\u25B2" },
  { label: "SYNCING ORBITAL MEMORY", icon: "\u25CF" },
  { label: "CALIBRATING AI MATRICES", icon: "\u2B22" },
  { label: "DEPLOYING ENERGY FIELDS", icon: "\u2726" },
  { label: "SYSTEM OPERATIONAL", icon: "\u2713" },
];
const GLITCH_CHARS = "!@#$%^&*01";

export default function HolographicLoader({ onComplete }: { onComplete: () => void }) {
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0);
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState(0);
  const [glitchText, setGlitchText] = useState("");
  const [fadeOut, setFadeOut] = useState(false);
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const rafRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const progressRef = useRef(0);
  const onCompleteRef = useRef(onComplete);
  onCompleteRef.current = onComplete;
  const particlesRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; vx: number; vy: number; size: number; opacity: number; color: string; targetX: number; targetY: number; }[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setProgress(prev => {
        const next = prev + 0.8 + Math.random() * 1.5;
        if (next >= 100) {
          clearInterval(interval);
          setTimeout(() => {
            setFadeOut(true);
            setTimeout(() => onCompleteRef.current(), 800);
          }, 600);
          return 100;
        }
        return next;
      });
    }, 30);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    progressRef.current = progress;
    const phaseIndex = Math.min(Math.floor(progress / (100 / BOOT_PHASES.length)), BOOT_PHASES.length - 1);
    setPhase(phaseIndex);
  }, [progress]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const len = 8 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 16);
      let text = "";
      for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
        text += GLITCH_CHARS[Math.floor(Math.random() * GLITCH_CHARS.length)];
      }
      setGlitchText(text);
      setTimeout(() => setGlitchText(""), 50);
    }, 200);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    if (!ctx) return;
    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
    const w = window.innerWidth;
    const h = window.innerHeight;
    canvas.width = w * dpr;
    canvas.height = h * dpr;
    canvas.style.width = w + "px";
    canvas.style.height = h + "px";
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    const colors = ["#22d3ee", "#a855f7", "#34d399", "#f472b6", "#fbbf24"];
    const cx = w / 2;
    const cy = h / 2;
    particlesRef.current = Array.from({ length: 120 }, () => ({
      x: Math.random() * w,
      y: Math.random() * h,
      vx: 0, vy: 0,
      size: 1 + Math.random() * 2,
      opacity: 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.5,
      color: colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)],
      targetX: cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100,
      targetY: cy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100,
    }));
    const animate = () => {
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
      const t = Date.now() * 0.001;
      const convergence = Math.min(progressRef.current / 100, 1);
      for (const p of particlesRef.current) {
        const tx = p.x + (p.targetX - p.x) * convergence * 0.02;
        const ty = p.y + (p.targetY - p.y) * convergence * 0.02;
        p.vx += (tx - p.x) * 0.001;
        p.vy += (ty - p.y) * 0.001;
        p.vx *= 0.98;
        p.vy *= 0.98;
        p.x += p.vx + Math.sin(t + p.x * 0.01) * 0.3;
        p.y += p.vy + Math.cos(t + p.y * 0.01) * 0.3;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.size, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fillStyle = p.color;
        ctx.globalAlpha = p.opacity * (1 - convergence * 0.5);
        ctx.fill();
      }
      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {
        for (let j = i + 1; j < particlesRef.current.length; j++) {
          const a = particlesRef.current[i];
          const b = particlesRef.current[j];
          const dx = a.x - b.x; const dy = a.y - b.y;
          const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
          if (dist < 100) {
            ctx.globalAlpha = (1 - dist / 100) * 0.1;
            ctx.strokeStyle = "#22d3ee";
            ctx.lineWidth = 0.3;
            ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(a.x, a.y); ctx.lineTo(b.x, b.y); ctx.stroke();
          }
        }
      }
      const glowRadius = 40 + convergence * 60;
      const glow = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, glowRadius);
      glow.addColorStop(0, "rgba(34, 211, 238, " + (0.15 + convergence * 0.2) + ")");
      glow.addColorStop(1, "rgba(34, 211, 238, 0)");
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ctx.fillStyle = glow;
      ctx.fillRect(cx - glowRadius, cy - glowRadius, glowRadius * 2, glowRadius * 2);
      rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    };
    animate();
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
  }, []);

  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * 42;
  const offset = circumference - (progress / 100) * circumference;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] flex items-center justify-center"
      style={{ background: "#020203", opacity: fadeOut ? 0 : 1, transition: "opacity 0.8s ease-out" }}
    >
      <canvas ref={canvasRef} className="absolute inset-0" style={{ opacity: 0.6 }} />
      <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col items-center gap-8">
        <div className="relative w-28 h-28">
          <svg width="112" height="112" className="rotate-[-90deg]">
            <circle cx="56" cy="56" r="42" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.05)" strokeWidth="2" fill="none" />
            <circle cx="56" cy="56" r="42" stroke="url(#loaderGrad)" strokeWidth="2.5" fill="none" strokeDasharray={circumference} strokeDashoffset={offset} strokeLinecap="round" style={{ transition: "stroke-dashoffset 0.1s ease-out" }} />
            <defs>
              <linearGradient id="loaderGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="0%">
                <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#22d3ee" />
                <stop offset="50%" stopColor="#a855f7" />
                <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#22d3ee" />
              </linearGradient>
            </defs>
          </svg>
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
            <span className="text-2xl font-black text-white font-mono" style={{ textShadow: "0 0 20px rgba(34,211,238,0.5)" }}>
              {Math.floor(progress)}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="text-center">
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-black tracking-tighter">
            <span className="text-white">Edu</span>
            <span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 to-indigo-500">AI.</span>
          </h1>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-mono text-cyan-400/80 tracking-widest">
            <span className="text-lg" style={{ filter: "drop-shadow(0 0 6px currentColor)" }}>{BOOT_PHASES[phase].icon}</span>
            <span>{BOOT_PHASES[phase].label}</span>
          </div>
          {glitchText && (
            <div className="absolute top-1/2 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-xs font-mono text-cyan-500/20 tracking-[0.3em] pointer-events-none">
              {glitchText}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="w-72 space-y-1 mt-4">
          {BOOT_PHASES.slice(0, phase + 1).map((p, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono" style={{ opacity: i === phase ? 1 : 0.3 }}>
              <span className={i < phase ? "text-emerald-500" : "text-cyan-400"}>{i < phase ? "[OK]" : "[..]"}</span>
              <span className="text-gray-500">{p.label}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="absolute w-64 h-[1px] bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-cyan-500/30 to-transparent" style={{ animation: "scan-line 2s linear infinite" }} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: app/components/KnowledgeGraph.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

interface Node { id: string; label: string; group: number; radius?: number; }
interface Link { source: string; target: string; value: number; }
interface KnowledgeGraphProps {
  data: { nodes: Node[]; links: Link[] };
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
}

export default function KnowledgeGraph({ data, width = 600, height = 400 }: KnowledgeGraphProps) {
  const ref = useRef<SVGSVGElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ref.current) return;
    const svg = ref.current;
    svg.innerHTML = '';

    // Simple force-directed layout (no d3 dependency)
    const nodes = data.nodes.map(n => ({ ...n, x: width / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200, y: height / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200, vx: 0, vy: 0 }));
    const links = data.links;

    const colors = ['#22d3ee', '#a855f7', '#ec4899'];
    let animFrame: number;

    function simulate() {
      // Simple force simulation
      for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
        for (let j = i + 1; j < nodes.length; j++) {
          const dx = nodes[j].x - nodes[i].x;
          const dy = nodes[j].y - nodes[i].y;
          const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) || 1;
          const force = -200 / (dist * dist);
          nodes[i].vx += dx / dist * force;
          nodes[i].vy += dy / dist * force;
          nodes[j].vx -= dx / dist * force;
          nodes[j].vy -= dy / dist * force;
        }
      }
      // Link attraction
      for (const link of links) {
        const source = nodes.find(n => n.id === link.source);
        const target = nodes.find(n => n.id === link.target);
        if (!source || !target) continue;
        const dx = target.x - source.x;
        const dy = target.y - source.y;
        const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) || 1;
        const force = (dist - 100) * 0.005;
        source.vx += dx / dist * force;
        source.vy += dy / dist * force;
        target.vx -= dx / dist * force;
        target.vy -= dy / dist * force;
      }
      // Center gravity
      for (const node of nodes) {
        node.vx += (width / 2 - node.x) * 0.001;
        node.vy += (height / 2 - node.y) * 0.001;
        node.vx *= 0.9;
        node.vy *= 0.9;
        node.x += node.vx;
        node.y += node.vy;
      }
      render();
      animFrame = requestAnimationFrame(simulate);
    }

    function render() {
      svg.innerHTML = '';
      // Links
      for (const link of links) {
        const source = nodes.find(n => n.id === link.source);
        const target = nodes.find(n => n.id === link.target);
        if (!source || !target) continue;
        const line = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'line');
        line.setAttribute('x1', String(source.x));
        line.setAttribute('y1', String(source.y));
        line.setAttribute('x2', String(target.x));
        line.setAttribute('y2', String(target.y));
        line.setAttribute('stroke', 'rgba(34,211,238,0.2)');
        line.setAttribute('stroke-width', String(Math.sqrt(link.value)));
        svg.appendChild(line);
      }
      // Nodes
      for (const node of nodes) {
        const g = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'g');
        g.setAttribute('transform', `translate(${node.x},${node.y})`);
        const circle = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'circle');
        circle.setAttribute('r', String(node.radius || 20));
        circle.setAttribute('fill', colors[node.group % 3]);
        circle.setAttribute('opacity', '0.8');
        g.appendChild(circle);
        const text = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'text');
        text.textContent = node.label;
        text.setAttribute('text-anchor', 'middle');
        text.setAttribute('fill', 'white');
        text.setAttribute('font-size', '10px');
        text.setAttribute('font-family', 'monospace');
        text.setAttribute('dy', '4');
        g.appendChild(text);
        svg.appendChild(g);
      }
    }

    simulate();
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(animFrame);
  }, [data, width, height]);

  return <svg ref={ref} width={width} height={height} className="w-full h-full" />;
}

FILE: app/components/MagneticCursor.tsx

"use client";
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

export default function MagneticCursor() {
  const dotRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const ringRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const pos = useRef({ x: -100, y: -100 });
  const ring = useRef({ x: -100, y: -100 });
  const rafRef = useRef<number>(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onMove = (e: MouseEvent) => { pos.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY }; };
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove, { passive: true });
    const lerp = (a: number, b: number, t: number) => a + (b - a) * t;
    
    const tick = () => {
      ring.current.x = lerp(ring.current.x, pos.current.x, 0.12);
      ring.current.y = lerp(ring.current.y, pos.current.y, 0.12);
      if (dotRef.current) dotRef.current.style.transform = `translate3d(${pos.current.x - 4}px, ${pos.current.y - 4}px, 0)`;
      if (ringRef.current) ringRef.current.style.transform = `translate3d(${ring.current.x - 20}px, ${ring.current.y - 20}px, 0)`;
      rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
      cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <>
      <div ref={dotRef} className="fixed top-0 left-0 z-[999] pointer-events-none w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-cyan-400 mix-blend-difference" style={{ willChange: 'transform' }} />
      <div ref={ringRef} className="fixed top-0 left-0 z-[998] pointer-events-none w-10 h-10 rounded-full border border-white/30 mix-blend-difference" style={{ willChange: 'transform' }} />
      <style>{`@media (pointer: coarse) { .magnetic-cursor { display: none !important; } }`}</style>
    </>
  );
}

"use client";
import Link from 'next/link';
import { SignInButton, SignUpButton, UserButton, useAuth } from '@clerk/nextjs';
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export default function Navbar() {
  const { userId } = useAuth();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [scrolled, setScrolled] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleScroll = () => {
      setScrolled(window.scrollY > 20);
    };
    window.addEventListener("scroll", handleScroll, { passive: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener("scroll", handleScroll);
  }, []);

  return (
    <nav className={`w-full fixed top-0 z-50 transition-all duration-500 ${
      scrolled 
        ? 'bg-[#020203]/80 backdrop-blur-xl border-b border-white/[0.06] shadow-[0_4px_30px_rgba(0,0,0,0.3)]' 
        : 'bg-transparent border-b border-transparent'
    }`}>
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-6 h-20 flex items-center justify-between">
        
        {/* 🔥 Main Logo 🔥 */}
        <Link href="/" className="group flex items-center gap-2.5 hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-300">
          <div className="relative w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-500/20 to-indigo-500/20 border border-white/[0.08] flex items-center justify-center group-hover:border-cyan-500/30 transition-all duration-300">
            <span className="text-sm font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 to-indigo-500">E</span>
            <div className="absolute inset-0 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-500/10 to-transparent opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-300" />
          </div>
          <span className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black tracking-tighter text-white">
            Edu<span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 to-indigo-500">AI.</span>
          </span>
        </Link>

        {/* 💻 Desktop Navigation Links (Premium Glassmorphism Pill) 💻 */}
        <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-2 rounded-full border border-white/[0.06] bg-white/[0.02] backdrop-blur-sm shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(255,255,255,0.01)]">
          {[
            { href: "/optimizer", label: "Optimizer", color: "cyan" },
            { href: "/flashcards", label: "Flashcards", color: "purple" },
            { href: "/analyzer", label: "Skill Gap", color: "pink" },
          ].map((link) => {
            const isActive = pathname?.startsWith(link.href);
            return (
              <Link 
                key={link.href}
                href={link.href} 
                className={`relative px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
                  isActive 
                    ? `text-${link.color}-400 drop-shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(var(--tw-shadow-color),0.8)] bg-white/[0.05]` 
                    : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/[0.03]'
                }`}
                style={isActive ? { 
                  color: link.color === 'cyan' ? '#22d3ee' : link.color === 'purple' ? '#c084fc' : '#f472b6',
                  textShadow: `0 0 8px ${link.color === 'cyan' ? 'rgba(34,211,238,0.8)' : link.color === 'purple' ? 'rgba(192,132,252,0.8)' : 'rgba(244,114,182,0.8)'}`
                } : {}}
              >
                {link.label}
                {isActive && (
                  <span 
                    className="absolute -bottom-px left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-4 h-0.5 rounded-full"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: link.color === 'cyan' ? '#22d3ee' : link.color === 'purple' ? '#c084fc' : '#f472b6' }}
                  />
                )}
              </Link>
            );
          })}
          
          <div className="w-px h-5 bg-white/[0.08] mx-1"></div>
          
          {userId && (
            <Link 
              href="/dashboard" 
              className={`px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
                pathname?.startsWith('/dashboard') 
                  ? 'text-cyan-400 bg-white/[0.05]' 
                  : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/[0.03]'
              }`}
            >
              Dashboard
            </Link>
          )}
          

        </div>

        {/* RIGHT SECTION: PRO Button + Auth Buttons */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 md:gap-5">
          
          {/* 🌟 NEW PRO BUTTON (Spinning Gradient with Pinging Dot) 🌟 */}
          <Link href="/billing">
            <button className="relative group overflow-hidden rounded-full p-[1.5px] focus:outline-none active:scale-95 transition-all duration-300 shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(168,85,247,0.2)] hover:shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(168,85,247,0.6)] hover:-translate-y-0.5">
              {/* Spinning Light Border */}
              <span className="absolute inset-[-1000%] animate-[spin_3s_linear_infinite] bg-[conic-gradient(from_90deg_at_50%_50%,#E2CBFF_0%,#393BB2_50%,#E2CBFF_100%)] opacity-60 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity" />
              
              {/* Inner Dark Pill */}
              <span className="relative flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#050505] px-4 md:px-5 py-1.5 text-xs md:text-sm font-black text-white transition-colors group-hover:bg-[#0a0a0f]">
                <span className="tracking-widest bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-400 to-pink-500 bg-clip-text text-transparent mr-2">PRO</span>
                
                {/* Pinging Dot */}
                <span className="relative flex h-2 w-2">
                  <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-fuchsia-400 opacity-75"></span>
                  <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-fuchsia-500 shadow-[0_0_10px_#d946ef]"></span>
                </span>
              </span>
            </button>
          </Link>

          {/* 🔐 AUTH BUTTONS 🔐 */}
          {!userId ? (
            <>
              <SignInButton mode="modal">
                <button className="hidden md:block text-sm font-bold text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition-all duration-300 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/[0.03]">
                  Log In
                </button>
              </SignInButton>
              <SignUpButton mode="modal">
                <button className="relative px-5 py-2 md:px-6 md:py-2.5 text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-black bg-white rounded-full hover:scale-105 active:scale-95 transition-all duration-300 shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(255,255,255,0.2)] hover:shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(255,255,255,0.4)] flex items-center gap-2 group">
                  Sign Up
                  <svg className="hidden md:block w-4 h-4 text-black group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" strokeWidth="2.5"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M13 10V3L4 14h7v7l9-11h-7z"></path></svg>
                </button>
              </SignUpButton>
            </>
          ) : (
            /* Logged in Avatar Ring Glow */
            <div className="p-[2px] rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-500/30 to-purple-500/30 hover:from-cyan-500/50 hover:to-purple-500/50 transition-all duration-300 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(255,255,255,0.08)] hover:shadow-[0_0_25px_rgba(255,255,255,0.15)]">
              <UserButton appearance={{ elements: { avatarBox: "w-8 h-8 md:w-9 md:h-9" } }} />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: app/components/NewsletterSubscribe.tsx

"use client";
import { useState, FormEvent } from 'react';
import { AnimatePresence, motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { CheckCircle2, Loader2, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function NewsletterSubscribe() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'loading' | 'success' | 'error'>('idle');
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');

  const handleSubmit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!email.trim()) return;
    setStatus('loading');
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/newsletter', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) {
        setStatus('success');
        setMessage(data.message || 'Subscribed successfully!');
        setEmail('');
      } else {
        setStatus('error');
        setMessage(data.error || 'Failed to subscribe');
      }
    } catch {
      setStatus('error');
      setMessage('Network error. Please try again.');
    }
    setTimeout(() => { setStatus('idle'); setMessage(''); }, 4000);
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-3 max-w-md mx-auto relative">
      <input
        type="email"
        value={email}
        onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
        placeholder="your@email.com"
        className="w-full bg-[#020203] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-5 py-3 text-white placeholder:text-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500/50 text-sm transition-colors"
        disabled={status === 'loading' || status === 'success'}
        required
      />
      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={status === 'loading' || status === 'success'}
        className="w-full md:w-auto px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-500 to-purple-600 text-white hover:shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(6,182,212,0.4)] hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-95 transition-all duration-300 whitespace-nowrap disabled:opacity-60 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
      >
        {status === 'loading' ? (
          <><Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> Subscribing</>
        ) : status === 'success' ? (
          <><CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" /> Subscribed!</>
        ) : (
          'Subscribe'
        )}
      </button>
      <AnimatePresence>
        {message && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
            className={`absolute -bottom-8 left-0 right-0 text-center text-xs font-medium ${status === 'success' ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-red-400'}`}
          >
            {status === 'success' ? (
              <span className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"><CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {message}</span>
            ) : (
              <span className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"><AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> {message}</span>
            )}
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </form>
  );
}
