"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useUser } from '@clerk/nextjs';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Navbar from '../components/Navbar';
import { Ticket, RefreshCcw, Clock, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, Search, ExternalLink } from 'lucide-react';
import UnifiedBackground from '../components/UnifiedBackground';
import { ErrorBoundary } from '../components/ErrorBoundary';

export default function Dashboard() {
  const { user, isSignedIn, isLoaded } = useUser();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [credits, setCredits] = useState<number>(0);
  const [planName, setPlanName] = useState('Free Trial');
  const [usageHistory, setUsageHistory] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [tickets, setTickets] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [refunds, setRefunds] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isLoaded && !isSignedIn) {
      router.push('/');
      return;
    }
    if (isLoaded && isSignedIn) {
      loadData();
    }
  }, [isLoaded, isSignedIn]);

  const loadData = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const [creditsRes, historyRes, ticketsRes, refundsRes] = await Promise.all([
        fetch('/api/user/credits'),
        fetch('/api/history'),
        fetch('/api/user/support'),
        fetch('/api/user/refunds'),
      ]);

      if (creditsRes.ok) {
        const data = await creditsRes.json();
        setCredits(data.credits || 0);
        setPlanName(data.plan_name || 'Free Trial');
      }
      if (historyRes.ok) {
        const data = await historyRes.json();
        setUsageHistory(data.history || []);
      }
      if (ticketsRes.ok) {
        const data = await ticketsRes.json();
        setTickets(data.tickets || []);
      }
      if (refundsRes.ok) {
        const data = await refundsRes.json();
        setRefunds(data.refunds || []);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Dashboard load error:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <ErrorBoundary>
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#020203] text-white font-sans relative overflow-hidden">
      <UnifiedBackground />

      <Navbar />

      <div className="pt-28 pb-20 px-6 max-w-6xl mx-auto relative z-10">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="mb-10">
          <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-black tracking-tight mb-2">
            Welcome back,{' '}
            <span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 to-purple-500">
              {user?.firstName || 'Scholar'}
            </span>
          </h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm font-mono">{user?.emailAddresses?.[0]?.emailAddress}</p>
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-20">
            <div className="animate-spin w-8 h-8 border-2 border-cyan-400 border-t-transparent rounded-full"></div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            {/* Credits Card */}
            <div className="relative max-w-2xl mb-10">
              <div className="absolute -inset-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-500/20 via-purple-500/20 to-cyan-500/20 rounded-3xl opacity-60 animate-pulse-soft"></div>
              <div className="relative bg-[#07070a] border border-white/10 rounded-3xl p-8">
                <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-start md:items-center justify-between gap-6">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-xs font-mono text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">Active Plan</p>
                    <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">
                      <span className="text-cyan-400">{planName}</span>
                    </h2>
                    <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-2">Credits are consumed by AI engine requests</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-center bg-white/[0.02] border border-white/5 px-8 py-6 rounded-2xl">
                    <span className="text-5xl font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-br from-white to-gray-500 font-mono">
                      {credits}
                    </span>
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-mono text-cyan-400 uppercase tracking-widest mt-1">Credits Left</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="mt-6 pt-6 border-t border-white/5 flex gap-3">
                  <a
                    href="/billing"
                    className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-widest bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-500 to-purple-600 text-white hover:shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(6,182,212,0.4)] hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-95 transition-all duration-300"
                  >
                    🚀 Upgrade Plan
                  </a>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Usage History */}
            <div className="bg-[#07070a] border border-white/5 rounded-3xl p-8">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
                <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-widest">Usage History</h2>
                <button onClick={loadData} className="text-xs text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 hover:scale-105 active:scale-95 transition-all duration-200">↻ Refresh</button>
              </div>

              {usageHistory.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center py-12">
                  <div className="text-4xl mb-3">📊</div>
                  <p className="text-gray-500 font-mono text-sm">No usage history yet</p>
                  <p className="text-gray-600 text-xs mt-1">Start using the AI tools to see your history</p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                  <table className="w-full text-sm">
                    <thead>
                      <tr className="text-gray-500 text-xs uppercase tracking-widest border-b border-white/5">
                        <th className="text-left py-3 px-2">Tool</th>
                        <th className="text-left py-3 px-2">Input</th>
                        <th className="text-left py-3 px-2">Date</th>
                        <th className="text-left py-3 px-2">Used Credits</th>
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                      {usageHistory.map((item: any) => (
                        <tr key={item.id} className="border-b border-white/5 hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors">
                          <td className="py-3 px-2">
                            <span className="text-xs font-mono bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-400 border border-cyan-500/20 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                              {item.tool_name}
                            </span>
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3 px-2 text-gray-300 max-w-[200px] truncate">
                            {typeof item.input === 'string' ? item.input.slice(0, 50) : JSON.stringify(item.input)?.slice(0, 50)}
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3 px-2 text-gray-500 text-xs">{item.created_at?.slice(0, 10)}</td>
                          <td className="py-3 px-2 font-mono">{item.credits_used || 1}</td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Support Tickets History */}
            <div className="bg-[#07070a] border border-white/5 rounded-3xl p-8 mt-6">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
                <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-cyan-400 uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Ticket className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  Your Support Tickets
                </h2>
                <a href="/support" className="text-xs text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 flex items-center gap-1">
                  New Ticket <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                </a>
              </div>

              {tickets.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center py-8">
                  <Ticket className="w-8 h-8 text-gray-600 mx-auto mb-3" />
                  <p className="text-gray-500 font-mono text-sm">No support tickets yet</p>
                  <a href="/support"className="inline-block mt-3 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-400 border border-cyan-500/20 hover:bg-cyan-500/20 hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-95 transition-all duration-200"
                    >
                      Submit a Ticket
                  </a>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {tickets.slice(0, 5).map((ticket: any) => {
                    const statusColors: Record<string, string> = {
                      open: 'text-red-400 bg-red-500/10 border-red-500/30',
                      in_progress: 'text-yellow-400 bg-yellow-500/10 border-yellow-500/30',
                      resolved: 'text-green-400 bg-green-500/10 border-green-500/30',
                      closed: 'text-gray-400 bg-gray-500/10 border-gray-500/30',
                    };
                    const sc = statusColors[ticket.status] || 'text-gray-400 bg-gray-500/10 border-gray-500/30';
                    return (
                      <div key={ticket.id} className="bg-[#020203] border border-white/5 rounded-xl p-4 hover:border-cyan-500/20 transition-all">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <span className={`text-[10px] font-mono font-bold uppercase px-2 py-0.5 rounded border ${sc}`}>
                              {ticket.status?.replace('_', ' ') || 'open'}
                            </span>
                            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-gray-500">#{ticket.tracking_id}</span>
                          </div>
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-600">{ticket.created_at?.slice(0, 10)}</span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white mb-1">{ticket.subject || 'No Subject'}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 line-clamp-2">{ticket.message}</p>
                        {ticket.admin_response && (
                          <div className="mt-2 p-2 rounded-lg bg-cyan-500/5 border border-cyan-500/10">
                            <p className="text-[10px] text-cyan-400 font-mono mb-1">Response:</p>
                            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">{ticket.admin_response}</p>
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                  {tickets.length > 5 && (
                    <a href="/support" className="block text-center text-xs text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 py-2">
                      View all {tickets.length} tickets
                    </a>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Refund Requests History */}
            <div className="bg-[#07070a] border border-white/5 rounded-3xl p-8 mt-6">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
                <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-emerald-400 uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-2">
                  <RefreshCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  Refund Requests
                </h2>
                <a href="/refund-policy" className="text-xs text-emerald-400 hover:text-emerald-300 flex items-center gap-1">
                  Request Refund <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                </a>
              </div>

              {refunds.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center py-8">
                  <RefreshCcw className="w-8 h-8 text-gray-600 mx-auto mb-3" />
                  <p className="text-gray-500 font-mono text-sm">No refund requests yet</p>
                  <a href="/refund-policy"className="inline-block mt-3 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20 hover:bg-emerald-500/20 hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-95 transition-all duration-200"
                    >
                      Request a Refund
                  </a>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {refunds.slice(0, 5).map((refund: any) => (
                    <div key={refund.id} className="bg-[#020203] border border-white/5 rounded-xl p-4 hover:border-emerald-500/20 transition-all">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <span className={`text-[10px] font-mono font-bold uppercase px-2 py-0.5 rounded border ${
                            refund.status === 'approved' ? 'text-green-400 bg-green-500/10 border-green-500/30' :
                            refund.status === 'denied' ? 'text-red-400 bg-red-500/10 border-red-500/30' :
                            'text-yellow-400 bg-yellow-500/10 border-yellow-500/30'
                          }`}>
                            {refund.status || 'pending'}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-gray-500">#{refund.id}</span>
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-600">{refund.created_at?.slice(0, 10)}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 line-clamp-1">{refund.reason}</p>
                          {refund.admin_note && (
                            <p className="text-[10px] text-amber-400 mt-1">Admin: {refund.admin_note}</p>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        {refund.amount > 0 && (
                          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white font-mono">₹{refund.amount}</span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                  {refunds.length > 5 && (
                    <a href="/refund-policy" className="block text-center text-xs text-emerald-400 hover:text-emerald-300 py-2">
                      View all {refunds.length} requests
                    </a>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {/* 🔐 DEVELOPER: YOUR ACCOUNT ID — Yahan se Clerk ID copy karein */}
        {isSignedIn && (
          <div className="mt-10 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500/5 via-yellow-500/10 to-amber-500/5 border-2 border-amber-500/30 rounded-3xl p-8 shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(251,191,36,0.08)]">
            <details className="group">
              <summary className="cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-3 text-base text-amber-300 uppercase tracking-widest font-bold select-none hover:text-amber-200 transition-colors">
                {/* Animated dot */}
                <span className="relative flex h-3 w-3 shrink-0">
                  <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-amber-400 opacity-75"></span>
                  <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-3 w-3 bg-amber-500"></span>
                </span>
                👑 Developer: Your Account ID
                <span className="text-amber-500/70 font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-normal">
                  (click to reveal)
                </span>
              </summary>
              <div className="mt-6 p-6 rounded-2xl bg-[#020203] border border-amber-500/20">
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mb-4">
                  <span className="text-2xl">📋</span>
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-sm font-bold text-amber-200 mb-1">
                      Step 1: Copy your Clerk ID below
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-mono">
                      Then set it in your <code className="text-amber-400 bg-amber-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">.env.local</code> file to unlock the admin panel.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-black/40 p-1 rounded-xl border border-amber-500/10">
                  <code className="flex-1 text-sm md:text-base font-mono text-amber-300 bg-black/60 px-5 py-4 rounded-lg border border-amber-500/20 select-all truncate">
                    {user?.id}
                  </code>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(user?.id || '')}
                    className="px-5 py-4 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-yellow-500 text-black hover:shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(251,191,36,0.3)] hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-95 transition-all duration-300 shrink-0"
                  >
                    📋 Copy ID
                  </button>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-5 p-4 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/5 border border-amber-500/10">
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 font-mono mb-2">
                    ➤ Add this to <code className="text-amber-400">.env.local</code>:
                  </p>
                  <pre className="text-xs font-mono text-amber-300/90 bg-black/60 px-4 py-3 rounded-lg border border-white/5">
ADMIN_USER_ID={user?.id}
NEXT_PUBLIC_ADMIN_USER_ID={user?.id}
                  </pre>
                </div>

                <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-600 mt-4 font-mono">
                  Current env: <code className="text-gray-400">{process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_ADMIN_USER_ID ? '✅ SET' : '❌ NOT SET'}</code>
                </p>
              </div>
            </details>        </div>
      )}
      </div>
    </div>
    </ErrorBoundary>
  );
}

export default function FaqPage() {

  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState('all');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

    setOpenIndex(openIndex === index ? null : index);
  };

    const matchesCategory = activeCategory === 'all' || faq.category === activeCategory;
    const matchesSearch = searchQuery === '' ||

      faq.answer.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase());

  });

              <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-purple-400 opacity-75"></span>

            Everything you need to know about EduAI. Can&apos;t find what you&apos;re looking for? <a href="/support" className="text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 underline underline-offset-4">Contact our team</a>.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Search Bar */}
        <div className="relative max-w-xl mx-auto mb-10">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-500/20 via-fuchsia-500/20 to-pink-500/20 rounded-2xl opacity-60 animate-pulse-soft"></div>
          <div className="relative flex items-center bg-[#07070a] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-2xl overflow-hidden focus-within:border-purple-500/30 transition-all duration-300">
            <HelpCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-gray-500 ml-5 shrink-0" />

            )}

        {/* Category Tabs */}

            return (
              <button
                key={cat.id}
                onClick={() => setActiveCategory(cat.id)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest transition-all duration-300 ${
                  activeCategory === cat.id

                <Icon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                {cat.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* FAQ Items */}
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {filteredFAQs.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-16 bg-[#07070a] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-2xl">
              <HelpCircle className="w-12 h-12 text-gray-600 mx-auto mb-4" />
              <p className="text-gray-500 font-mono text-sm">No matching questions found</p>
              <button onClick={() => { setSearchQuery(''); setActiveCategory('all'); }} className="text-cyan-400 text-xs mt-2 hover:text-cyan-300 transition-colors">

                  <div className="px-6 pb-6 text-gray-400 leading-relaxed border-t border-white/[0.06] pt-4 ml-12">
                    {faq.answer}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Still Have Questions CTA */}
        <div className="mt-16 relative group">

          <div className="relative bg-[#07070a] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-3xl p-10 md:p-12 text-center">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-4 mb-6">
              <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-purple-500/10 border border-purple-500/30 flex items-center justify-center">
                <MessageCircle className="w-7 h-7 text-purple-400" />
              </div>
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold text-white mb-3">Still have questions?</h3>

================================================================================
FILE: app/flashcards/page.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react';
import Navbar from '../components/Navbar';
import { useUser } from '@clerk/nextjs';
import AmbientBackground from '../components/AmbientBackground';
import { ErrorBoundary } from '../components/ErrorBoundary';

interface Flashcard {
  front: string;
  back: string;
}

export default function FlashcardGenerator() {
  const { user } = useUser();
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [cards, setCards] = useState<Flashcard[]>([]);
  const [flipped, setFlipped] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleGenerate = async () => {
    setError(null);
    if (!text.trim()) {
      setError("SYSTEM ALERT: Please enter some text to generate flashcards!");
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    setCards([]);
    setFlipped(new Set());
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/flashcards', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ text, userId: user?.id }),
      });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error);
      setCards(data.flashcards);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Matrix Overload");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const toggleFlip = (index: number) => {
    setFlipped(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      next.has(index) ? next.delete(index) : next.add(index);
      return next;
    });
  };

  return (
    <ErrorBoundary>
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#020203] text-white relative selection:bg-cyan-500/30 font-sans pb-32 overflow-hidden">
      
      {/* Premium Antigravity & Liquid Background */}
      <div className="fixed inset-0 pointer-events-none -z-10">
        <div className="absolute top-0 left-1/4 w-[1000px] h-[1000px] rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-900/10 via-transparent to-cyan-900/10 blur-[160px] animate-liquid-bg"></div>
        <div className="absolute bottom-[-10%] right-1/4 w-[600px] h-[600px] rounded-full bg-cyan-950/20 blur-[120px]" style={{ animationDelay: '2s' }}></div>
      </div>

      <Navbar />

      <div className="pt-36 px-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto relative z-10">
        
        {/* Header Section */}
        <div className="text-center mb-20">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-3 px-5 py-2 rounded-full bg-[#0a0a0c] border border-white/[0.06] text-sm font-medium mb-6 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(168,85,247,0.1)]">
            <span className="relative flex h-2.5 w-2.5">
              <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-purple-400 opacity-75"></span>
              <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2.5 w-2.5 bg-purple-500"></span>
            </span>
            <span className="text-gray-200 tracking-wider uppercase font-semibold">Memory Neural Engine</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-6xl md:text-8xl font-extrabold tracking-tighter mb-5">
            Flashcard <span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-400 via-fuchsia-300 to-cyan-500">Forge.</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="text-gray-400 max-w-3xl mx-auto text-xl leading-relaxed">Inject raw knowledge into the system. Our neural matrix constructs multidimensional, interactive memory units for instant recall.</p>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="mb-4 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/50 text-red-400 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-mono flex items-center gap-3 animate-pulse">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 bg-red-500 rounded-full shrink-0"></span>
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Floating Input Section (Glassmorphism) */}
        <div className="relative group max-w-4xl mx-auto mb-20 animate-float">
          <div className="absolute -inset-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600/30 to-cyan-500/30 rounded-3xl blur opacity-30 group-hover:opacity-60 transition duration-1000"></div>
          <div className="bg-[#0a0a0c]/90 backdrop-blur-3xl rounded-[2rem] p-8 border border-white/[0.05] relative shadow-2xl">
            <textarea 
              value={text}
              onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full h-[160px] bg-transparent text-gray-100 placeholder:text-gray-600 focus:outline-none resize-none text-xl leading-relaxed" 
              placeholder="Paste your chaotic notes here... Physics, Coding, History... the Forge handles everything..."
            ></textarea>
            
            {/* BUTTONS CONTAINER (Import on left, Generate on right) */}
            <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-white/5 pt-6">
              
              {/* Techy Import Button */}
              <button className="group relative inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-all duration-300 bg-white/5 border border-white/10 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/10 hover:border-white/20 active:scale-95 overflow-hidden">
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-500/20 to-cyan-500/20 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-300"></div>
                <svg 
                  className="w-4 h-4 transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:-translate-y-1 group-hover:text-cyan-400" 
                  fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"
                >
                  <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M4 16v1a3 3 0 003 3h10a3 3 0 003-3v-1m-4-8l-4-4m0 0L8 8m4-4v12" />
                </svg>
                <span className="relative z-10">Import Data</span>
              </button>

              {/* Main Generate Button */}
              <button 
                onClick={handleGenerate}
                disabled={loading}
                className={`px-10 py-4 rounded-xl font-extrabold text-sm uppercase tracking-wider transition-all duration-300 relative overflow-hidden group
                ${loading ? 'bg-white/10 text-gray-500 cursor-not-allowed' : 'bg-white text-black hover:scale-[1.03] shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(255,255,255,0.1)] hover:shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(168,85,247,0.3)]'}`}
              >
                {loading ? (
                    <span className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3">
                        <svg className="animate-spin h-5 w-5 text-purple-600" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle className="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="4"></circle><path className="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path></svg>
                        Forging Neural Cards...
                    </span>
                ) : (
                    <span className="relative z-10 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                        Initialize Matrix ✨
                    </span>
                )}
              </button>
            </div>

          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 3D Liquid Flashcards Grid */}
        {cards.length > 0 && (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-10">
            {cards.map((card, index) => (
              <div 
                key={index} 
                className="group relative h-[320px] w-full cursor-pointer perspective-2000 animate-fade-in-up"
                style={{ animationDelay: `${index * 150}ms` }}
                onClick={() => toggleFlip(index)}
              >
                {/* Floating Antigravity Effect on Hover */}
                <div className={`w-full h-full transition-transform duration-[800ms] transform-style-3d group-hover:-translate-y-4 ${flipped.has(index) ? 'rotate-y-180' : ''}`}>
                  
                  {/* Front of Card (Frosted Glass + Liquid Glow) */}
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 backface-hidden bg-gradient-to-br from-[#111] to-[#0a0a0c] border border-white/[0.08] rounded-3xl p-8 flex flex-col justify-center items-center text-center shadow-xl group-hover:border-purple-500/40 group-hover:shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(168,85,247,0.15)] transition-all duration-500 overflow-hidden">
                    {/* Liquid Glow Overlay */}
                    <div className="absolute -inset-20 bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600/10 via-cyan-500/10 to-purple-600/10 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 animate-liquid-card transition-opacity duration-1000"></div>
                    
                    <span className="absolute top-5 left-5 text-xs font-mono font-bold text-purple-400 bg-purple-500/10 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-purple-500/20">DATAUNIT_{index + 1}</span>
                    <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white leading-snug z-10 relative">{card.front}</h3>
                    <p className="absolute bottom-5 text-xs text-gray-600 z-10 font-medium">Click Matrix to Reveal</p>
                  </div>

                  {/* Back of Card (Reverse Liquid + Neon Border) */}
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 backface-hidden rotate-y-180 bg-[#08080a] border border-cyan-500/30 rounded-3xl p-8 flex flex-col justify-center items-center text-center shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(6,182,212,0.15)] overflow-hidden">
                     {/* Reverse Liquid Glow */}
                    <div className="absolute -inset-20 bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-600/10 via-purple-500/10 to-cyan-600/10 animate-liquid-card transition-opacity duration-1000"></div>

                    <span className="absolute top-5 left-5 text-xs font-mono font-bold text-cyan-400 bg-cyan-500/10 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-cyan-500/20">NEURAL_DECODE</span>
                    <p className="text-xl font-medium text-gray-100 leading-relaxed z-10 relative">{card.back}</p>
                    <p className="absolute bottom-5 text-xs text-cyan-700 z-10">Neural Injection Complete</p>
                  </div>

                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

    </div>
    </ErrorBoundary>
  )
}

FILE: app/hooks/useGPUTier.ts
================================================================================

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
export type GPUTier = 'low' | 'medium' | 'high';
export function useGPUTier(): GPUTier {
  const [tier, setTier] = useState<GPUTier>('medium');
  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    const gl = canvas.getContext('webgl');
    if (!gl) { setTier('low'); return; }
    const debug = gl.getExtension('WEBGL_debug_renderer_info');
    if (!debug) { setTier('medium'); return; }
    const renderer = gl.getParameter(debug.UNMASKED_RENDERER_WEBGL);
    if (/(Mali-G|Adreno 3|Intel HD)/i.test(renderer)) setTier('low');
    else if (/(RTX|GTX 1[0-9]|Apple M)/i.test(renderer)) setTier('high');
  }, []);
  return tier;
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/hooks/useMagnetic.ts
================================================================================

'use client';
import { useRef, useEffect } from 'react';

export function useMagnetic(strength = 0.3) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const onMove = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      const cx = rect.left + rect.width / 2;
      const cy = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
      el.style.transform = `translate(${(e.clientX - cx) * strength}px, ${(e.clientY - cy) * strength}px)`;
    };
    const onLeave = () => {
      el.style.transform = 'translate(0,0)';
      el.style.transition = 'transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1)';
    };
    el.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
    el.addEventListener('mouseleave', onLeave);
    return () => { el.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove); el.removeEventListener('mouseleave', onLeave); };
  }, [strength]);
  return ref;
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/hooks/useParallax.ts
================================================================================

'use client';
import { useEffect } from 'react';

export function useParallax() {
  useEffect(() => {
    const onScroll = () => {
      const y = window.scrollY;
      document.querySelectorAll('.parallax-slow').forEach(el => { (el as HTMLElement).style.transform = `translate3d(0, ${y * 0.15}px, 0)`; });
      document.querySelectorAll('.parallax-fast').forEach(el => { (el as HTMLElement).style.transform = `translate3d(0, ${y * 0.3}px, 0)`; });
    };
    window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll);
  }, []);
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/hooks/useReducedMotion.ts
================================================================================

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export function useReducedMotion() {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const mq = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
    setReduced(mq.matches);
    const handler = (e: MediaQueryListEvent) => setReduced(e.matches);
    mq.addEventListener('change', handler);
    return () => mq.removeEventListener('change', handler);
  }, []);
  return reduced;
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/hooks/useScrollReveal.ts
================================================================================

'use client';
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

export function useScrollReveal(threshold = 0.1) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const obs = new IntersectionObserver(([entry]) => {
      if (entry.isIntersecting) { el.classList.add('animate-reveal-up'); el.style.opacity = '1'; obs.unobserve(el); }
    }, { threshold });
    obs.observe(el);
    return () => obs.disconnect();
  }, [threshold]);
  return ref;
}

FILE: app/not-found.tsx

"use client";
import Link from 'next/link';
import Navbar from './components/Navbar';
import UnifiedBackground from './components/UnifiedBackground';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

const GLITCH_CHARS = "!@#$%^&*()_+-=[]{}|;:,.<>?/~`0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz";

// Pre-generate stable matrix rain columns (won't flicker on re-render)
const matrixColumns = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, i) => ({
  left: i * 5,
  speed: 3 + (((i * 7 + 3) % 11) / 11) * 4,
  delay: (((i * 13 + 5) % 17) / 17) * 3,
  chars: Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, j) => GLITCH_CHARS[((i * 31 + j * 17 + 7) % GLITCH_CHARS.length)]),
}));

export default function NotFound() {
  const [glitchText, setGlitchText] = useState("404");

  useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setGlitchText("404".split("").map((char) => 
        Math.random() > 0.7 ? GLITCH_CHARS[Math.floor(Math.random() * GLITCH_CHARS.length)] : char
      ).join(""));
    }, 100);
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => setGlitchText("404"), 200);
    return () => { clearInterval(interval); clearTimeout(timeout); };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#020203] text-white font-sans relative overflow-hidden">
      <UnifiedBackground />
      <Navbar />

      {/* Matrix rain effect - stable characters */}
      <div className="fixed inset-0 pointer-events-none z-[1] overflow-hidden opacity-10">
        {matrixColumns.map((col, i) => (
          <div
            key={i}
            className="absolute text-[10px] font-mono text-cyan-400 whitespace-nowrap"
            style={{
              left: `${col.left}%`,
              animation: `matrix-fall ${col.speed}s linear infinite`,
              animationDelay: `${col.delay}s`,
            }}
          >
            {col.chars.map((char, j) => (
              <div key={j} className="leading-5">{char}</div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-screen px-6 relative z-10">
        <div className="text-center">
          {/* Glitch 404 */}
          <div className="relative mb-8">
            <div className="text-[150px] md:text-[220px] font-black tracking-tighter leading-none text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-b from-white/30 to-white/[0.03] select-none glitch-text" data-text={glitchText}>
              {glitchText}
            </div>
            <div className="absolute inset-0 text-[150px] md:text-[220px] font-black tracking-tighter leading-none text-cyan-400/10 select-none glitch-layer-1" aria-hidden="true">404</div>
            <div className="absolute inset-0 text-[150px] md:text-[220px] font-black tracking-tighter leading-none text-fuchsia-400/10 select-none glitch-layer-2" aria-hidden="true">404</div>
          </div>

          <div className="w-full max-w-md mx-auto h-[1px] bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-cyan-500/40 to-transparent mb-8 animate-scan-horizontal" />

          <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-5xl font-black tracking-tight mb-4">
            Lost in the{' '}
            <span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 via-indigo-400 to-purple-500">
              Matrix
            </span>
          </h1>

          <p className="text-gray-500 text-lg max-w-md mx-auto mb-10">
            The page you&apos;re looking for doesn&apos;t exist or has been moved to another dimension.
          </p>

          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-4">
            <Link
              href="/"
              className="group relative px-8 py-4 rounded-xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-500 to-blue-600 text-white overflow-hidden transition-all duration-300 hover:shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(6,182,212,0.5)] hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-95"
            >
              <span className="relative z-10 flex items-center gap-2">
                Return Home
                <svg className="w-4 h-4 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" strokeWidth="2.5"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M5 12h14m-7-7l7 7-7 7" /></svg>
              </span>
              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-cyan-500 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-300" />
            </Link>
            <Link
              href="/optimizer"
              className="group px-8 py-4 rounded-xl font-bold border border-white/10 text-gray-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5 hover:border-white/20 transition-all duration-300 flex items-center gap-2"
            >
              Go to Optimizer
              <svg className="w-4 h-4 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24" strokeWidth="2.5"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M5 12h14m-7-7l7 7-7 7" /></svg>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>


    </div>
  );
}

FILE: app/optimizer/page.tsx

"use client";
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import Navbar from '../components/Navbar';
import MagneticCursor from '../components/MagneticCursor';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabaseClient';
import { useUser } from '@clerk/nextjs';
import UnifiedBackground from '../components/UnifiedBackground';
import { ErrorBoundary } from '../components/ErrorBoundary';

// Types
interface TimetableSlot {
  time: string;
  slotTitle: string;
  details: string;
}

interface DayProtocol {
  day: number;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  timetable: TimetableSlot[];
}

export default function Optimizer() {
  const { user } = useUser();
  const [syllabus, setSyllabus] = useState('');
  const [days, setDays] = useState('');
  const [isGenerating, setIsGenerating] = useState(false);
  const [protocol, setProtocol] = useState<DayProtocol[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [completedDays, setCompletedDays] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());

  const toggleComplete = (day: number) => {
    setCompletedDays(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      next.has(day) ? next.delete(day) : next.add(day);
      return next;
    });
  };

  const handleGenerate = async () => {
    if (!syllabus.trim() || !days) {
      setError("SYSTEM ALERT: Please input both Syllabus and Target Days.");
      return;
    }

    setIsGenerating(true);
    setError('');
    setProtocol([]);

    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/generate', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ syllabus, days, userId: user?.id }),
      });

      const data = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.error || "Neural Engine connection failed.");
      }

      // ✅ FIX: Backend 'protocol' (array) use kar, purana 'plan' (string) nahi
      const planData: DayProtocol[] = data.protocol;
      setProtocol(planData);

      // Save to Supabase
      const { error: dbError } = await supabase
        .from('study_plans')
        .insert([{ syllabus, days, plan: JSON.stringify(planData) }]);

      if (dbError) console.error("Supabase Save Error:", dbError);

    } catch (err: unknown) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "An unknown error occurred.");
    } finally {
      setIsGenerating(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <ErrorBoundary>
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#030305] text-white font-sans relative overflow-x-hidden selection:bg-cyan-500/30">

      {/* Background Glows */}
      <div className="fixed inset-0 pointer-events-none z-0 flex justify-center items-center">
        <div className="w-[70vw] h-[70vw] md:w-[40vw] md:h-[40vw] rounded-full bg-cyan-900/10 blur-[150px] animate-pulse-slow"></div>
        <div className="absolute top-1/4 right-0 w-[50vw] h-[50vw] bg-purple-900/10 rounded-full blur-[150px] animate-pulse-slow delay-1000"></div>
      </div>

      <MagneticCursor />
      <Navbar />

      <div className="pt-32 pb-20 px-4 md:px-8 max-w-5xl mx-auto relative z-10 flex flex-col min-h-[90vh]">

        {/* Header */}
        <div className="mb-12 text-center animate-fade-in-up">
          <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-6xl font-black tracking-tighter mb-4 uppercase drop-shadow-2xl">
            Curriculum <span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 to-blue-600">Analyzer.</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm md:text-base font-light tracking-wide max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            Input raw syllabus text or upload documents. The AI will deconstruct it into an optimized, bite-sized daily execution protocol.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Input Terminal */}
        <div className="cyber-box p-[1.5px] rounded-2xl mb-12 animate-fade-in-up" style={{ animationDelay: '0.1s' }}>
          <div className="relative bg-[#07070a]/95 backdrop-blur-2xl rounded-2xl p-6 border border-white/5 flex flex-col gap-6">

            {error && (
              <div className="bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/50 text-red-400 px-4 py-3 rounded-lg text-sm font-mono flex items-center gap-3 animate-pulse">
                <span className="w-2 h-2 bg-red-500 rounded-full"></span>
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-6">
              <div className="flex-grow space-y-2">
                <label className="text-xs font-mono text-cyan-400 uppercase tracking-widest ml-1">Raw Syllabus Input</label>
                <textarea
                  value={syllabus}
                  onChange={(e) => setSyllabus(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Paste your syllabus here..."
                  className="w-full h-32 bg-[#000000] border border-white/10 rounded-xl p-4 text-white placeholder:text-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500/50 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-cyan-500/50 transition-all font-mono text-sm resize-none custom-scrollbar"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="md:w-64 flex flex-col gap-4">
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <label className="text-xs font-mono text-purple-400 uppercase tracking-widest ml-1">Target Days</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    value={days}
                    onChange={(e) => setDays(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="e.g. 30"
                    className="w-full bg-[#000000] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder:text-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500/50 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-purple-500/50 transition-all font-mono text-sm"
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <label className="text-xs font-mono text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest ml-1">Import Data</label>
                  <label className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 w-full bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-dashed border-white/20 hover:border-cyan-500/50 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-gray-300 hover:text-cyan-400 cursor-pointer transition-all">
                    <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" d="M4 16v1a3 3 0 003 3h10a3 3 0 003-3v-1m-4-8l-4-4m0 0L8 8m4-4v12"></path></svg>
                    <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Upload PDF/IMG</span>
                    <input type="file" className="hidden" accept=".pdf,image/*" onChange={() => alert("File upload connected!")} />
                  </label>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={handleGenerate}
              disabled={isGenerating}
              className={`w-full py-4 rounded-xl font-black tracking-widest uppercase transition-all duration-300 flex items-center justify-center gap-3 ${isGenerating ? 'bg-gray-800 text-gray-500 cursor-not-allowed' : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-500 to-blue-500 text-white hover:shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(6,182,212,0.6)] hover:scale-[1.01] active:scale-95'}`}
            >
              {isGenerating ? (
                <>
                  <span className="animate-spin w-5 h-5 border-2 border-white/20 border-t-white rounded-full"></span>
                  ANALYZING NEURAL DATA...
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  INITIALIZE ANALYZER
                  <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="3" d="M13 10V3L4 14h7v7l9-11h-7z"></path></svg>
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ✅ FIXED: Protocol Display — JSON array se render karta hai */}
        {protocol.length > 0 && (
          <div className="animate-fade-in-up" style={{ animationDelay: '0.2s' }}>

            {/* Header */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-8">
              <div className="h-[1px] flex-grow bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent to-cyan-500/50"></div>
              <span className="text-cyan-400 font-mono text-xs tracking-[0.2em] uppercase border border-cyan-500/30 px-4 py-1 rounded-full bg-cyan-500/10 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(6,182,212,0.2)] flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="w-2 h-2 bg-cyan-400 rounded-full animate-ping"></span>
                PROTOCOL DEPLOYED — {protocol.length} DAYS
              </span>
              <div className="h-[1px] flex-grow bg-gradient-to-l from-transparent to-cyan-500/50"></div>
            </div>

            {/* Day Cards */}
            <div className="relative border-l-2 border-cyan-900/50 ml-4 md:ml-8 space-y-8 pb-10">
              {protocol.map((dayItem, index) => (
                <div
                  key={dayItem.day}
                  className="relative pl-8 md:pl-12 animate-fade-in-right"
                  style={{ animationDelay: `${index * 0.08}s` }}
                >
                  {/* Timeline dot */}
                  <div className={`absolute -left-[9px] top-4 w-4 h-4 bg-[#020203] border-2 rounded-full shadow-[0_0_15px_#06b6d4] transition-all ${completedDays.has(dayItem.day) ? 'border-green-400 shadow-[0_0_15px_#4ade80]' : 'border-cyan-400'}`}></div>

                  <div className={`group relative bg-[#07070a]/80 backdrop-blur-xl border rounded-2xl p-6 transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 ${completedDays.has(dayItem.day) ? 'border-green-500/30 hover:border-green-500/50 hover:shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(74,222,128,0.15)]' : 'border-white/5 hover:border-cyan-500/50 hover:shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(6,182,212,0.15)]'}`}>

                    {/* Day number + title */}
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between mb-3">
                      <div>
                        <span className="text-xs font-mono text-gray-500 tracking-widest uppercase">DAY {dayItem.day}</span>
                        <h3 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 to-blue-500 tracking-wider">
                          {dayItem.title}
                        </h3>
                      </div>
                      {completedDays.has(dayItem.day) && (
                        <span className="text-xs font-mono text-green-400 border border-green-500/30 px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-green-500/10">✓ DONE</span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    {/* Description */}
                    <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm md:text-base leading-relaxed mb-4">
                      {dayItem.description}
                    </p>

                    {/* Timetable slots */}
                    {dayItem.timetable && dayItem.timetable.length > 0 && (
                      <div className="space-y-2 mb-4">
                        <p className="text-xs font-mono text-purple-400 uppercase tracking-widest">Timetable</p>
                        {dayItem.timetable.map((slot, i) => (
                          <div key={i} className="flex gap-3 bg-white/3 border border-white/5 rounded-xl p-3">
                            <span className="text-xs font-mono text-cyan-500 whitespace-nowrap pt-0.5">{slot.time}</span>
                            <div>
                              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white">{slot.slotTitle}</p>
                              <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">{slot.details}</p>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {/* Complete button */}
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-t border-white/5 pt-4">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={completedDays.has(dayItem.day)}
                        onChange={() => toggleComplete(dayItem.day)}
                        className="w-5 h-5 accent-cyan-500 rounded bg-[#000] border-gray-600 cursor-pointer"
                      />
                      <span className="text-xs font-mono text-cyan-500 tracking-widest uppercase cursor-pointer" onClick={() => toggleComplete(dayItem.day)}>
                        Mark as Completed
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>


    </div>
    </ErrorBoundary>
  );
}

import { useParallax } from "./hooks/useParallax";
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";

const CARDS = [
  {
    href: "/optimizer",
    accent: "#06b6d4",
    accentRgb: "6, 182, 212",
    tag: "Module 01",
    title: "Curriculum Engine",
    desc: "Automated synthesis of dense academic structures into executable daily protocols.",

    accentRgb: "236, 72, 153",
    tag: "Module 03",
    title: "Skill Diagnostic",
    desc: "Cross-referencing personal capabilities against global industry requirements.",
    icon: "M9 19v-6a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v6a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2zm0 0V9a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2v10m-6 0a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2m0 0V5a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2v14a2 2 0 01-2 2h-2a2 2 0 01-2-2z",
  },
];

export default function Home() {
  const cardsRef = useRef<(HTMLAnchorElement | null)[]>([]);
  useParallax();

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleMouseMove = (e: MouseEvent, card: HTMLElement) => {
      const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
      const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
      const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
      
      // Spotlight position
      card.style.setProperty("--mouse-x", `${x}px`);
      card.style.setProperty("--mouse-y", `${y}px`);
      
      // 3D tilt physics (subtle, premium feel)
      const centerX = rect.width / 2;
      const centerY = rect.height / 2;
      const rotateX = ((y - centerY) / centerY) * -6; // max 6deg
      const rotateY = ((x - centerX) / centerX) * 6;
      
      card.style.setProperty("--rx", `${rotateX}deg`);
      card.style.setProperty("--ry", `${rotateY}deg`);
    };

    const handleMouseLeave = (card: HTMLElement) => {
      card.style.setProperty("--rx", "0deg");
      card.style.setProperty("--ry", "0deg");
      card.style.setProperty("--mouse-x", "-999px");
      card.style.setProperty("--mouse-y", "-999px");
    };

    cardsRef.current.forEach((card) => {
      if (!card) return;
      const onMove = (e: MouseEvent) => handleMouseMove(e, card);
      card.addEventListener("mousemove", onMove);
      card.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => handleMouseLeave(card));
    });

    return () => {
      cardsRef.current.forEach((card) => {
        if (!card) return;
        // cleanup handled by closure, but safe to leave as is since component unmounts
      });
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <>
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-[var(--bg-primary,#020203)] text-white font-sans relative overflow-hidden selection:bg-white/20">
        
        <UnifiedBackground />
        <MagneticCursor />
        <Navbar />
        <OnboardingTour />

      <div className="pt-32 pb-24 px-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto text-center relative z-10 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-screen">
        
        {/* Hero typography with tighter tracking */}
        <h1 
          className="text-[clamp(2.5rem,6vw,5.5rem)] font-black tracking-tight leading-[1.05] mb-7 parallax-slow"
          style={{ animation: 'hero-weight 4s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1) infinite alternate' }}
        >
          <span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-300 via-indigo-300 to-fuchsia-400 animate-gradient-x">
            EduAI.
          </span>
        </h1>

        <p className="text-white/40 text-lg md:text-xl max-w-xl mx-auto mb-20 font-light tracking-wide leading-relaxed">
          Advanced algorithmic deconstruction of your curriculum and career path.
        </p>

        {/* PREMIUM CARDS GRID */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-7 max-w-6xl mx-auto text-left w-full perspective-[2000px]">
          {CARDS.map((card, i) => (
            <Link
              key={i}
              href={card.href}
              ref={(el) => { cardsRef.current[i] = el; }}
              className="group relative block h-[320px] rounded-3xl overflow-hidden cursor-pointer card-enter"
              style={{ 
                animationDelay: `${0.1 + i * 0.15}s`,
                "--card-accent": card.accent,
                "--card-accent-rgb": card.accentRgb,
              } as React.CSSProperties}
            >
              {/* Card base with glass and depth */}
              <div className="absolute inset-0 rounded-3xl bg-white/[0.02] backdrop-blur-xl border border-white/[0.06] shadow-[inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.05),0_25px_50px_-12px_rgba(0,0,0,0.5)] transition-all duration-500 ease-out group-hover:border-white/[0.12] group-hover:shadow-[inset_0_1px_1px_rgba(255,255,255,0.1),0_30px_60px_-12px_rgba(0,0,0,0.6),0_0_0_1px_rgba(var(--card-accent-rgb),0.15)]" />
              
              {/* Ambient glow behind card (always visible but subtle) */}
              <div 
                className="absolute -inset-px rounded-3xl opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-700 blur-xl -z-10"
                style={{ background: `radial-gradient(circle at 50% 0%, ${card.accent}20, transparent 70%)` }}
              />

              {/* Mouse-tracking spotlight overlay */}
              <div 
                className="absolute inset-0 rounded-3xl opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-300 pointer-events-none z-20"
                style={{
                  background: `radial-gradient(600px circle at var(--mouse-x, -999px) var(--mouse-y, -999px), rgba(var(--card-accent-rgb), 0.08), transparent 40%)`
                }}
              />

              {/* 3D Tilt container */}
              <div 
                className="absolute inset-[1px] rounded-[22px] overflow-hidden z-10 transition-transform duration-100 ease-out will-change-transform"
                style={{
                  transform: "perspective(1000px) rotateX(var(--rx, 0deg)) rotateY(var(--ry, 0deg)) translateZ(20px)"
                }}
              >
                {/* Inner gradient background */}
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-b from-white/[0.03] to-transparent" />
                
                {/* Content */}
                <div className="relative h-full p-7 flex flex-col">
                  
                  {/* Icon with ambient glow */}
                  <div className="relative mb-5">
                    <div 
                      className="absolute inset-0 rounded-xl blur-md opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-40 transition-opacity duration-500"
                      style={{ background: card.accent }}
                    />
                    <div className="relative w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.04] border border-white/[0.08] flex items-center justify-center group-hover:border-white/[0.15] group-hover:bg-white/[0.07] transition-all duration-300">
                      <svg width="20" height="20" fill="none" stroke={card.accent} strokeWidth="1.5" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d={card.icon} />
                      </svg>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Tag */}
                  <div 
                    className="text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase mb-2.5 opacity-50 group-hover:opacity-80 transition-opacity duration-300"
                    style={{ color: card.accent }}
                  >
                    {card.tag}
                  </div>

                  {/* Title */}
                  <h3 className="text-[1.35rem] font-bold text-white/90 tracking-tight mb-3 group-hover:text-white transition-colors duration-300">
                    {card.title}
                  </h3>

                  {/* Description */}
                  <p className="text-[0.85rem] text-white/30 leading-[1.7] flex-grow group-hover:text-white/45 transition-colors duration-300">
                    {card.desc}
                  </p>

                  {/* Footer / Deploy */}
                  <div className="mt-auto pt-5 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-white/[0.06] group-hover:border-white/[0.1] transition-colors duration-300">
                    <span 
                      className="text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.15em] uppercase transition-all duration-300 group-hover:tracking-[0.25em]"
                      style={{ color: card.accent }}
                    >
                      Deploy
                    </span>
                    
                    <div className="relative w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-white/[0.05] border border-white/[0.08] flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden group-hover:border-white/[0.15] group-hover:bg-white/[0.1] transition-all duration-300">
                      <svg 
                        className="w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:translate-x-0.5" 
                        fill="none" 
                        stroke={card.accent} 
                        strokeWidth="2.5" 
                        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                      >
                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M5 12h14m-7-7l7 7-7 7" />
                      </svg>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Holographic sheen sweep on hover */}
                <div className="absolute inset-0 -translate-x-full group-hover:translate-x-full transition-transform duration-1000 ease-in-out bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-white/[0.03] to-transparent skew-x-12 pointer-events-none" />
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      </div>

      <Testimonials />

      <Footer />

            <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                <Shield className="w-6 h-6 text-emerald-400" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white mb-2">TL;DR — Our Commitment</h2>
                <p className="text-gray-400 leading-relaxed">
                  We collect only what is necessary to provide our AI study tools. We never sell your data. Your study plans, flashcards, and analysis are yours — we are just the engine. All sensitive operations are handled by trusted third-party providers (Clerk, Razorpay) who meet the highest security standards.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Sections */}
        <div className="space-y-8">
          {SECTIONS.map((section, i) => {
            const Icon = section.icon;
            return (
              <div key={i} className={`group bg-[#07070a]/80 border border-white/[0.06] rounded-2xl p-8 md:p-10 transition-all duration-500 ${colorBorderMap[section.color]}`}>
                <div className="flex items-start gap-4 mb-6">
                  <div className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl border flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 transition-all duration-300 ${colorIconBg[section.color]}`}>
                  
                    <Icon className={`w-6 h-6 ${colorAccent[section.color]}`} />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">{section.title}</h2>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <p className="text-gray-400 leading-relaxed mb-4">{section.content}</p>
                <ul className="space-y-3">
                  {section.items.map((item, j) => (
                    <li key={j} className="flex items-start gap-3 text-gray-400">
                      <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full mt-2 shrink-0 ${colorAccent[section.color]}`}
                        style={{ backgroundColor: 'currentColor' }}
                      />
                      <span className="text-sm leading-relaxed">{item}</span>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Footer CTA */}
        <div className="mt-16 text-center bg-[#07070a] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-3xl p-10 md:p-12">
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-3">Questions about your data?</h3>
          <p className="text-gray-500 mb-6 max-w-md mx-auto">
            Our data protection team is ready to help with any privacy concerns or data requests.
          </p>
          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-4">
            <a
              href="mailto:privacy@eduai.com"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-8 py-4 rounded-xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-500 to-blue-600 text-white hover:shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(6,182,212,0.4)] hover:scale-[1.02] transition-all duration-300 active:scale-95"
            >
              <Mail className="w-4 h-4" />
              Contact DPO
