"use client";
import { useState } from 'react';
import Navbar from '../components/Navbar';
import AmbientBackground from '../components/AmbientBackground';
import { RefreshCcw, CheckCircle2, XCircle, Clock, Mail, ArrowUpRight, AlertTriangle, Send, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

const STEPS = [
  { icon: Mail, title: 'Submit Request', desc: 'Fill out the refund request form below with your order details and reason.' },
  { icon: Clock, title: 'Under Review', desc: 'Our team reviews your request within 2-3 business days.' },
  { icon: CheckCircle2, title: 'Approved / Denied', desc: 'You will receive an email with the decision and any admin notes.' },
  { icon: RefreshCcw, title: 'Refund Processed', desc: 'If approved, funds are credited to your original payment method within 5-7 days.' },
];

export default function RefundPolicy() {
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    email: '',
    orderId: '',
    amount: '',
    reason: '',
  });
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!formData.email || !formData.reason) {
      setError('Please fill in all required fields.');
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/refund', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          user_email: formData.email,
          order_id: formData.orderId,
          amount: formData.amount ? parseInt(formData.amount) : 0,
          reason: formData.reason,
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error);
      setSubmitted(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Failed to submit refund request');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#020203] text-white font-sans relative overflow-hidden">
      <AmbientBackground />

      <Navbar />

      <div className="relative z-10 pt-36 pb-20 px-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
        
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="mb-16 text-center">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-3 px-5 py-2 rounded-full bg-white/[0.04] border border-white/[0.08] text-xs font-bold tracking-widest mb-6 animate-shimmer-overlay shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(16,185,129,0.1)]">
            <RefreshCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
            <span className="text-gray-300 uppercase">Refund & Cancellation</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-5xl md:text-7xl font-black tracking-tight mb-4 uppercase">
            <span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-400 to-green-500">Refund</span> Policy
          </h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            Last updated: January 2025 &nbsp;·&nbsp; We strive to be fair and transparent with all refund requests.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Process Steps */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-16">
          {STEPS.map((step, i) => {
            const Icon = step.icon;
            return (
              <div key={i} className="bg-[#07070a] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-6 text-center hover:border-emerald-500/20 transition-all duration-300">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
                  <Icon className="w-6 h-6 text-emerald-400" />
                </div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white mb-1">{step.title}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 leading-relaxed">{step.desc}</p>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Policy Content + Form */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-8 mb-16">
          
          {/* Policy Details */}
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <div className="bg-[#07070a] border border-emerald-500/20 rounded-2xl p-8">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-emerald-400 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5" />
                Eligible for Refund
              </h2>
              <ul className="space-y-3">
                {[
                  'Request made within 7 days of purchase',
                  'Less than 10% of purchased credits consumed',
                  'Account in good standing',
                  'Standard credit pack purchases (not promotional)',
                ].map((item, i) => (
                  <li key={i} className="flex items-start gap-3 text-sm text-gray-400">
                    <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 mt-1.5 shrink-0" />
                    {item}
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-[#07070a] border border-red-500/20 rounded-2xl p-8">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-red-400 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                <XCircle className="w-5 h-5" />
                Not Eligible
              </h2>
              <ul className="space-y-3">
                {[
                  'Purchases made more than 7 days ago',
                  'Credits that have been consumed for AI generation',
                  'Promotional, discounted, or free trial credits',
                  'Accounts with a history of multiple refund requests',
                ].map((item, i) => (
                  <li key={i} className="flex items-start gap-3 text-sm text-gray-400">
                    <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-red-500 mt-1.5 shrink-0" />
                    {item}
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-[#07070a] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-2xl p-8">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-cyan-400 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Clock className="w-5 h-5" />
                Processing Timeline
              </h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 leading-relaxed">
                Refund requests are reviewed within <strong className="text-white">2-3 business days</strong>. 
                If approved, the amount is credited to your original payment method within <strong className="text-white">5-7 business days</strong>. 
                You will receive email notifications at every stage of the process.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Refund Request Form */}
          <div className="relative group">
            <div className="absolute -inset-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600/20 via-green-500/20 to-emerald-600/20 rounded-3xl opacity-60 group-hover:opacity-90 transition duration-700"></div>
            <div className="relative bg-[#07070a] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-3xl p-8">
              
              {submitted ? (
                <div className="text-center py-10">
                  <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/30 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-6">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-8 h-8 text-emerald-400" />
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-3">Request Submitted!</h3>
                  <p className="text-gray-400 mb-6 max-w-sm mx-auto">
                    Your refund request has been received. You will receive an email update within 2-3 business days.
                  </p>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => { setSubmitted(false); setFormData({ email: '', orderId: '', amount: '', reason: '' }); }}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold bg-white/5 border border-white/10 text-gray-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 transition-all"
                  >
                    Submit Another Request
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
                    <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 flex items-center justify-center">
                      <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-400" />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Request a Refund</h2>
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">All fields marked * are required</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {error && (
                    <div className="mb-4 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 text-red-400 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm flex items-center gap-3">
                      <XCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                      {error}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">
                        Email Address *
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="email"
                        value={formData.email}
                        onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, email: e.target.value })}
                        placeholder="your@email.com"
                        className="w-full bg-[#020203] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder:text-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500/50 transition-all text-sm"
                        required
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">
                          Order ID
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={formData.orderId}
                          onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, orderId: e.target.value })}
                          placeholder="Optional"
                          className="w-full bg-[#020203] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder:text-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500/50 transition-all text-sm"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">
                          Amount (₹)
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          value={formData.amount}
                          onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, amount: e.target.value })}
                          placeholder="Optional"
                          className="w-full bg-[#020203] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder:text-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500/50 transition-all text-sm"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">
                        Reason for Refund *
                      </label>
                      <textarea
                        value={formData.reason}
                        onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, reason: e.target.value })}
                        placeholder="Please describe why you are requesting a refund..."
                        rows={4}
                        className="w-full bg-[#020203] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder:text-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500/50 transition-all text-sm resize-none"
                        required
                      />
                    </div>

                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      disabled={submitting}
                      className="w-full py-4 rounded-xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-green-600 text-white hover:shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(16,185,129,0.4)] hover:scale-[1.02] transition-all duration-300 active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                    >
                      {submitting ? (
                        <><Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> Submitting...</>
                      ) : (
                        <><Send className="w-4 h-4" /> Submit Refund Request</>
                      )}
                    </button>
                  </form>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

        {/* Contact CTA */}
        <div className="mt-8 bg-[#07070a] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-3xl p-10 text-center">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-4 mb-4">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-cyan-500/10 border border-cyan-500/20 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Mail className="w-6 h-6 text-cyan-400" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-white mb-2">Need help with your refund request?</h3>
          <p className="text-gray-500 mb-6 max-w-md mx-auto">
            Contact our support team for any questions about the refund process.
          </p>
          <a
            href="/support"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-500 to-blue-600 text-white hover:shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(6,182,212,0.4)] transition-all"
          >
            Contact Support <ArrowUpRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </a>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: app/sitemap.ts

import { MetadataRoute } from "next";

export default function sitemap(): MetadataRoute.Sitemap {
  const baseUrl = "https://eduai.vercel.app";
  const pages = [
    "", "/optimizer", "/flashcards", "/analyzer",
    "/about", "/blog", "/faq",
    "/privacy-policy", "/terms-of-service", "/refund-policy",
    "/billing",
  ];

  const blogPosts = [
    "/blog/ai-study-tips-2025",
    "/blog/curriculum-optimization-guide",
    "/blog/flashcard-memory-retention",
    "/blog/skill-gap-analysis-career",
  ];

  const allRoutes = [...pages, ...blogPosts];

  return allRoutes.map(route => ({
    url: baseUrl + route,
    lastModified: new Date(),
    changeFrequency: route === "" ? "weekly" as const : "monthly" as const,
    priority: route === "" ? 1 : route.startsWith("/blog/") ? 0.7 : 0.8,
  }));
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/support/page.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react';
import Navbar from '../components/Navbar';
import AmbientBackground from '../components/AmbientBackground';
import { 
  MessageCircle, Send, Search, Package, 
  CheckCircle2, Clock, AlertCircle, Loader2, 
  ArrowUpRight, ChevronDown, Ticket
} from 'lucide-react';

export default function SupportPage() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'submit' | 'track'>('submit');
  
  // Submit ticket form
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [subject, setSubject] = useState('');
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [submitResult, setSubmitResult] = useState<{ success: boolean; trackingId?: string; error?: string } | null>(null);

  // Track ticket form
  const [trackingId, setTrackingId] = useState('');
  const [trackEmail, setTrackEmail] = useState('');
  const [tracking, setTracking] = useState(false);
  const [ticketData, setTicketData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [trackError, setTrackError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmitTicket = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!email || !message) return;
    setSubmitting(true);
    setSubmitResult(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/support', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email, subject, message }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error);
      setSubmitResult({ success: true, trackingId: data.tracking_id });
    } catch (err: any) {
      setSubmitResult({ success: false, error: err.message });
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleTrackTicket = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!trackingId) return;
    setTracking(true);
    setTrackError(null);
    setTicketData(null);
    try {
      const params = new URLSearchParams({ id: trackingId });
      if (trackEmail) params.append('email', trackEmail);
      const res = await fetch(`/api/support/track?${params}`);
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.error);
      setTicketData(data.ticket);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setTrackError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setTracking(false);
    }
  };

  const statusConfig: Record<string, { color: string; bg: string; border: string; icon: any }> = {
    open: { color: 'text-red-400', bg: 'bg-red-500/10', border: 'border-red-500/30', icon: AlertCircle },
    in_progress: { color: 'text-yellow-400', bg: 'bg-yellow-500/10', border: 'border-yellow-500/30', icon: Clock },
    resolved: { color: 'text-green-400', bg: 'bg-green-500/10', border: 'border-green-500/30', icon: CheckCircle2 },
    closed: { color: 'text-gray-400', bg: 'bg-gray-500/10', border: 'border-gray-500/30', icon: CheckCircle2 },
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#020203] text-white relative overflow-hidden">
      <AmbientBackground />

      <Navbar />

      <div className="relative z-10 max-w-5xl mx-auto px-6 pt-36 pb-24">

        {/* Header */}
        <div className="text-center mb-12">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-3 px-5 py-2 rounded-full bg-white/[0.04] border border-white/[0.08] text-xs font-bold tracking-widest mb-6 animate-shimmer-overlay shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(6,182,212,0.1)]">
            <MessageCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-400" />
            <span className="text-gray-300 uppercase">Help Center</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-5xl md:text-7xl font-black tracking-tighter mb-4 uppercase">
            <span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 via-indigo-400 to-purple-500">Support</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500 max-w-2xl mx-auto text-lg">
            Need help? Submit a ticket or track an existing request using your tracking ID.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Tab Switcher */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mb-12">
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('submit')}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold transition-all duration-300 ${
              activeTab === 'submit'
                ? 'bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-400 border border-cyan-500/30 shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(6,182,212,0.15)]'
                : 'bg-white/[0.03] text-gray-500 border border-white/[0.06] hover:text-white hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-95'
            }`}
          >
            <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
            Submit Ticket
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('track')}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold transition-all duration-300 ${
              activeTab === 'track'
                ? 'bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-400 border border-cyan-500/30 shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(6,182,212,0.15)]'
                : 'bg-white/[0.03] text-gray-500 border border-white/[0.06] hover:text-white hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-95'
            }`}
          >
            <Search className="w-4 h-4" />
            Track Request
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Submit Ticket Form */}
        {activeTab === 'submit' && (
          <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            <div className="relative group">
              <div className="absolute -inset-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-600/20 via-indigo-500/20 to-purple-600/20 rounded-3xl opacity-60 group-hover:opacity-90 transition duration-700"></div>
              <div className="relative bg-[#07070a] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-3xl p-8">
                
                {submitResult?.success ? (
                  <div className="text-center py-10">
                    <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-green-500/10 border border-green-500/30 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-6">
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-8 h-8 text-green-400" />
                    </div>
                    <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-2">Ticket Created!</h3>
                    <div className="inline-block bg-[#020203] border border-cyan-500/20 rounded-xl px-6 py-4 mb-4">
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-mono mb-1">Your Tracking ID</p>
                      <p className="text-2xl font-black text-cyan-400 font-mono tracking-wider">{submitResult.trackingId}</p>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-gray-400 mb-6 text-sm">
                      A confirmation email has been sent to <strong className="text-white">{email}</strong>. Use the tracking ID to check your ticket status.
                    </p>
                    <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-3">
                      <button onClick={() => { setActiveTab('track'); setTrackingId(submitResult.trackingId || ''); }}
                                                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-400 border border-cyan-500/30 hover:bg-cyan-500/30 hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-95 transition-all duration-200"
                      >
                        Track Now <Search className="w-4 h-4" />
                      </button>
                      <button onClick={() => { setSubmitResult(null); setEmail(''); setSubject(''); setMessage(''); }}
                                                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold bg-white/5 border border-white/10 text-gray-300 hover:text-white hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-95 transition-all duration-200"
                      >
                        New Ticket
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
                      <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-cyan-500/10 border border-cyan-500/20 flex items-center justify-center">
                        <Ticket className="w-5 h-5 text-cyan-400" />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Submit a Support Ticket</h2>
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">We typically respond within 24 hours</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {submitResult?.error && (
                      <div className="mb-4 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 text-red-400 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm flex items-center gap-3">
                        <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                        {submitResult.error}
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <form onSubmit={handleSubmitTicket} className="space-y-5">
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Email *</label>
                        <input
                          type="email"
                          value={email}
                          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="your@email.com"
                          className="w-full bg-[#020203] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder:text-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500/50 text-sm"
                          required
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Subject</label>
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={subject}
                          onChange={(e) => setSubject(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="Brief title for your issue"
                          className="w-full bg-[#020203] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder:text-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500/50 text-sm"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Message *</label>
                        <textarea
                          value={message}
                          onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="Describe your issue in detail..."
                          rows={5}
                          className="w-full bg-[#020203] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder:text-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500/50 text-sm resize-none"
                          required
                        />
                      </div>
                      <button
                        type="submit"
                        disabled={submitting}
                        className="w-full py-4 rounded-xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-500 to-blue-600 text-white hover:shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(6,182,212,0.4)] hover:scale-[1.02] transition-all duration-300 active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                      >
                        {submitting ? (
                          <><Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> Submitting...</>
                        ) : (
                          <><Send className="w-4 h-4" /> Submit Ticket</>
                        )}
                      </button>
                    </form>
                  </>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Track Ticket */}
        {activeTab === 'track' && (
          <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            <div className="bg-[#07070a] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-3xl p-8 mb-8">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-purple-500/10 border border-purple-500/20 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <Search className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-400" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">Track Your Request</h2>
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Enter the tracking ID from your confirmation email</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <form onSubmit={handleTrackTicket} className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Tracking ID *</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={trackingId}
                    onChange={(e) => setTrackingId(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
                    placeholder="EDU-XXXXXXXX"
                    className="w-full bg-[#020203] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder:text-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500/50 text-sm font-mono uppercase tracking-wider"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Email (for verification)</label>
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    value={trackEmail}
                    onChange={(e) => setTrackEmail(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="your@email.com (optional)"
                    className="w-full bg-[#020203] border border-white/10 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder:text-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-purple-500/50 text-sm"
                  />
                </div>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={tracking || !trackingId}
                  className="w-full py-4 rounded-xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-500 to-pink-600 text-white hover:shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(168,85,247,0.4)] hover:scale-[1.02] transition-all duration-300 active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                >
                  {tracking ? (
                    <><Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> Searching...</>
                  ) : (
                    <><Search className="w-4 h-4" /> Track Request</>
                  )}
                </button>
              </form>
            </div>

            {/* Ticket Status Result */}
            {trackError && (
              <div className="bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 rounded-2xl p-6 text-center">
                <AlertCircle className="w-10 h-10 text-red-400 mx-auto mb-3" />
                <p className="text-red-400 font-bold mb-1">Ticket Not Found</p>
                <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm">{trackError}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-3">Check your tracking ID and try again, or submit a new ticket.</p>
              </div>
            )}

            {ticketData && (
              <div className="bg-[#07070a] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-3xl overflow-hidden">
                {/* Status Header */}
                <div className={`p-6 border-b border-white/[0.06] flex items-center justify-between ${
                  ticketData.status === 'resolved' ? 'bg-green-500/5' : 
                  ticketData.status === 'in_progress' ? 'bg-yellow-500/5' : 'bg-red-500/5'
                }`}>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <span className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest border ${
                      statusConfig[ticketData.status]?.color || 'text-gray-400'
                    } ${statusConfig[ticketData.status]?.bg || 'bg-gray-500/10'} ${statusConfig[ticketData.status]?.border || 'border-gray-500/30'}`}>
                      {ticketData.status?.replace('_', ' ') || 'open'}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-mono">#{ticketData.tracking_id}</span>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-xs text-gray-600">{new Date(ticketData.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                </div>

                <div className="p-6 space-y-4">
                  {ticketData.subject && (
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-mono mb-1">Subject</p>
                      <p className="text-white font-bold">{ticketData.subject}</p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-mono mb-1">Message</p>
                    <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">{ticketData.message}</p>
                  </div>
                  {ticketData.admin_response && (
                    <div className="bg-cyan-500/5 border border-cyan-500/15 rounded-xl p-4">
                      <p className="text-xs text-cyan-400 font-mono mb-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                        <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                        Admin Response
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-gray-300 text-sm">{ticketData.admin_response}</p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-4 pt-2 text-xs text-gray-600">
                    <span>Created: {new Date(ticketData.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                    {ticketData.updated_at && (
                      <span>Updated: {new Date(ticketData.updated_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Quick Links */}
        <div className="mt-16 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 max-w-3xl mx-auto">
          {[
            { icon: MessageCircle, label: 'FAQ', desc: 'Browse common questions', href: '/faq' },
            { icon: Package, label: 'Refund Policy', desc: 'Request a refund', href: '/refund-policy' },
            { icon: ArrowUpRight, label: 'Dashboard', desc: 'View your usage & credits', href: '/dashboard' },
          ].map((item, i) => {
            const Icon = item.icon;
            return (
              <a key={i} href={item.href} className="group bg-[#07070a] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl p-5 hover:border-cyan-500/20 hover:scale-[1.02] active:scale-95 transition-all duration-300 text-center block">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-cyan-500/10 border border-cyan-500/20 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-3">
                  <Icon className="w-5 h-5 text-cyan-400" />
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white mb-1 group-hover:text-cyan-400 transition-colors">{item.label}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{item.desc}</p>
              </a>
            );
          })}
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



================================================================================
FILE: app/template.tsx
================================================================================

"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import HolographicLoader from "./components/HolographicLoader";

const pageVariants = {
  initial: {
    opacity: 0,
    y: 20,
    filter: "blur(8px)",
  },
  animate: {
    opacity: 1,
    y: 0,
    filter: "blur(0px)",
    transition: {
      duration: 0.5,
      ease: "easeOut" as const,
    },
  },
  exit: {
    opacity: 0,
    y: -10,
    filter: "blur(4px)",
    transition: {
      duration: 0.3,
      ease: "easeIn" as const,
    },
  },
};

export default function Template({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Only show loader on first visit, not on route changes
    const hasVisited = sessionStorage.getItem("eduai-booted");
    if (hasVisited) {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const handleBootComplete = () => {
    sessionStorage.setItem("eduai-booted", "1");
    setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <>
      {loading && <HolographicLoader onComplete={handleBootComplete} />}
      <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
        <motion.div
          key={pathname}
          variants={pageVariants}
          initial="initial"
          animate="animate"
          exit="exit"
          className="min-h-screen"
        >
          {children}
        </motion.div>
      </AnimatePresence>
    </>
  );
}

  children,
}: Readonly<{
  children: React.ReactNode;
}>) {
  return <>{children}</>;
}

};

export default function TermsOfService() {
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#020203] text-white font-sans relative overflow-hidden">
      <AmbientBackground />

      <Navbar />

      <div className="relative z-10 pt-36 pb-20 px-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
        
        <div className="mb-16 text-center">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-3 px-5 py-2 rounded-full bg-white/[0.04] border border-white/[0.08] text-xs font-bold tracking-widest mb-6 animate-shimmer-overlay shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(167,139,250,0.1)]">
            <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-400" />
            <span className="text-gray-300 uppercase">Legal Agreement</span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-5xl md:text-7xl font-black tracking-tight mb-4 uppercase">
            <span className="text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-400 to-pink-500">Terms</span> of Service
          </h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            Last updated: January 2025 &nbsp;·&nbsp; Please read these terms carefully before using EduAI.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Summary Card */}
        <div className="relative group mb-12">
          <div className="absolute -inset-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-500/10 via-pink-500/10 to-indigo-500/10 rounded-3xl opacity-60 animate-pulse-soft"></div>
          <div className="relative bg-[#07070a] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-3xl p-8 md:p-10">
            <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-purple-500/10 border border-purple-500/20 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                <FileText className="w-6 h-6 text-purple-400" />
              </div>
              <div>

                <p className="text-gray-400 leading-relaxed">
                  These terms govern your use of EduAI. By using our platform, you agree to follow these rules. 
                  We provide AI study tools on a credit-based system. Your data is handled according to our 
                  <a href="/privacy-policy" className="text-cyan-400 hover:underline mx-1">Privacy Policy</a>. 
                  If you have questions, contact our support team.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Sections */}
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {SECTIONS.map((section, i) => {
            const Icon = section.icon;
            return (
              <div key={i} className={`group bg-[#07070a]/80 border border-white/[0.06] rounded-2xl p-8 md:p-10 transition-all duration-500 ${colorBorderMap[section.color]}`}>
                <div className="flex items-start gap-4 mb-4">
                  <div className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl border flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 transition-all duration-300 ${colorIconBg[section.color]}`}>
                    <Icon className={`w-6 h-6 ${colorAccent[section.color]}`} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="pt-1">
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">{section.title}</h2>
                  </div>
                </div>
                {section.content && (
                  <p className="text-gray-400 leading-relaxed">{section.content}</p>
                )}
                {section.items && (
                  <ul className="space-y-3 mt-2">
                    {section.items.map((item, j) => (
                      <li key={j} className="flex items-start gap-3 text-gray-400">
                        <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full mt-2 shrink-0 ${colorAccent[section.color]}`}
                          style={{ backgroundColor: 'currentColor' }}
                        />
                        <span className="text-sm leading-relaxed">{item}</span>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Contact CTA */}
        <div className="mt-12 text-center bg-[#07070a] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-3xl p-10 md:p-12">
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-3">Questions about these terms?</h3>
          <p className="text-gray-500 mb-6 max-w-md mx-auto">
            If you have any questions about these Terms of Service, please contact our support team.
          </p>
          <a
            href="/support"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-8 py-4 rounded-xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-500 to-pink-600 text-white hover:shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(168,85,247,0.4)] hover:scale-[1.02] transition-all duration-300 active:scale-95"
          >

            <ArrowUpRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </a>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );

FILE: lib/adminAuth.ts

// ===== CLIENT-SIDE HELPERS =====
export function getStoredAdminPassword(): string {
  const hash = localStorage.getItem('admin_token_hash');
  if (!hash) return '';
  try {
    return atob(hash).split(':')[0] || '';
  } catch {
    return '';
  }
}

export function setAdminPassword(password: string): void {
  const salt = Date.now().toString(36);
  const hash = btoa(password + ':' + salt);
  localStorage.setItem('admin_token_hash', hash);
  localStorage.setItem('admin_expiry', (Date.now() + 4 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toString());
}

export function clearAdminAuth(): void {
  localStorage.removeItem('admin_token_hash');
  localStorage.removeItem('admin_expiry');
}

export function isAdminSessionValid(): boolean {
  const expiry = localStorage.getItem('admin_expiry');
  return !!expiry && parseInt(expiry) > Date.now();
}

export function hashToken(pwd: string): string {
  const salt = Date.now().toString(36);
  return btoa(pwd + ':' + salt);
}

// ===== SERVER-SIDE (PURANA — API routes ke liye) =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';

export async function verifyAdminAuth(req: Request): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization');
    if (!authHeader?.startsWith('Bearer ')) return false;
    
    const token = authHeader.split(' ')[1];
    const adminPassword = process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD;
    
    if (!adminPassword) return false;
    
    // Simple comparison (replace with bcrypt in production)
    return token === adminPassword;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export function unauthorizedResponse() {
  return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
}

FILE: lib/credits.ts

'use server';
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';

const supabaseAdmin = createClient(
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!
);

export async function checkAndDeductCredit(userId: string): Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string; remaining?: number }> {
  try {
    const { data: user, error: fetchError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from('user_credits')
      .select('credits')
      .eq('user_id', userId)
      .single();
    
    if (fetchError || !user) return { ok: false, error: 'User not found' };
    if (user.credits <= 0) return { ok: false, error: 'CREDIT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED' };
    
    const newCredits = user.credits - 1;
    const { error: updateError } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from('user_credits')
      .update({ credits: newCredits, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
      .eq('user_id', userId);
    
    if (updateError) return { ok: false, error: 'Update failed' };
    return { ok: true, remaining: newCredits };
  } catch {
    return { ok: true };
  }
}

export async function logUserHistory(userId: string, toolName: string, input: string, creditsUsed = 1) {
  try {
    await supabaseAdmin.from('user_history').insert({
      user_id: userId,
      tool_name: toolName,
      input: input.slice(0, 500),
      credits_used: creditsUsed,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  } catch {}
}

================================================================================
FILE: lib/rateLimit.ts
================================================================================

import { LRUCache } from 'lru-cache';

const rateLimitCache = new LRUCache<string, number[]>({
  max: 500,
  ttl: 1000 * 60 * 60,
});

export function rateLimit(ip: string, limit: number = 10, windowMs: number = 60000): boolean {
  const now = Date.now();
  const timestamps = rateLimitCache.get(ip) || [];
  const windowStart = now - windowMs;
  const recent = timestamps.filter(t => t > windowStart);
  
  if (recent.length >= limit) return false;
  
  recent.push(now);
  rateLimitCache.set(ip, recent);
  return true;
}

FILE: lib/usageLimits.ts

'use client';

// Anonymous usage gates using localStorage
// Free users (non-logged-in) get limited daily usage

const STORAGE_KEYS = {
  DAILY_USAGE: 'eduai_daily_usage',
  FIRST_VISIT: 'eduai_first_visit',
  ONBOARDING_DONE: 'eduai_onboarding_done',
};

// Daily limits for anonymous users
const DAILY_LIMITS: Record<string, number> = {
  optimizer: 2,
  flashcards: 3,
  analyzer: 2,
};

// Daily limits for logged-in free users
const FREE_USER_LIMITS: Record<string, number> = {
  optimizer: 5,
  flashcards: 10,
  analyzer: 5,
};

function getTodayKey(): string {
  const d = new Date();
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${d.getMonth() + 1}-${d.getDate()}`;
}

function getUsage(): Record<string, { date: string; counts: Record<string, number> }> {
  try {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.DAILY_USAGE);
    if (stored) return JSON.parse(stored);
  } catch {}
  return {};
}

function saveUsage(usage: Record<string, { date: string; counts: Record<string, number> }>) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.DAILY_USAGE, JSON.stringify(usage));
  } catch {}
}

export function checkUsageLimit(tool: string, isLoggedIn: boolean): { allowed: boolean; remaining: number } {
  const today = getTodayKey();
  const usage = getUsage();
  const limits = isLoggedIn ? FREE_USER_LIMITS : DAILY_LIMITS;
  const limit = limits[tool] || 3;

  if (!usage[today]) {
    usage[today] = { date: today, counts: {} };
  }

  const currentCount = usage[today].counts[tool] || 0;
  const remaining = Math.max(0, limit - currentCount);

  return {
    allowed: currentCount < limit,
    remaining,
  };
}

export function incrementUsage(tool: string) {
  const today = getTodayKey();
  const usage = getUsage();

  if (!usage[today]) {
    usage[today] = { date: today, counts: {} };
  }

  usage[today].counts[tool] = (usage[today].counts[tool] || 0) + 1;
  saveUsage(usage);
}

export function getDailyResetTime(): string {
  const now = new Date();
  const tomorrow = new Date(now);
  tomorrow.setDate(tomorrow.getDate() + 1);
  tomorrow.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  const diffMs = tomorrow.getTime() - now.getTime();
  const hours = Math.floor(diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60));
  const minutes = Math.floor((diffMs % (1000 * 60 * 60)) / (1000 * 60));
  return `${hours}h ${minutes}m`;
}

// Onboarding helpers
export function isOnboardingDone(): boolean {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.ONBOARDING_DONE) === 'true';
  } catch {
    return true;
  }
}

export function setOnboardingDone() {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.ONBOARDING_DONE, 'true');
  } catch {}
}

export function isFirstVisit(): boolean {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.FIRST_VISIT) === null;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export function markVisited() {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.FIRST_VISIT, 'true');
  } catch {}
}

================================================================================
FILE: scripts/fix_cleanup.py

import pathlib

path = pathlib.Path("app/page.tsx")
content = path.read_text()

old_effect = '''    cardsRef.current.forEach((card) => {
      if (!card) return;
      const onMove = (e: MouseEvent) => handleMouseMove(e, card);
      card.addEventListener("mousemove", onMove);
      card.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => handleMouseLeave(card));
    });
  }, []);'''

new_effect = '''    const cleanups: (() => void)[] = [];
    cardsRef.current.forEach((card) => {
      if (!card) return;
      const onMove = (e: MouseEvent) => handleMouseMove(e, card);
      const onLeave = () => handleMouseLeave(card);
      card.addEventListener("mousemove", onMove);
      card.addEventListener("mouseleave", onLeave);
      cleanups.push(() => {
        card.removeEventListener("mousemove", onMove);
        card.removeEventListener("mouseleave", onLeave);
      });
    });
    return () => cleanups.forEach((fn) => fn());
  }, []);'''

if old_effect in content:
    content = content.replace(old_effect, new_effect)
    path.write_text(content)
    print("Fixed cleanup successfully")
else:
    print("ERROR: Old effect text not found!")
    print("Looking for:", repr(old_effect[:80]))

FILE: scripts/run_migration.js
================================================================================

// Script to run SQL migration on Supabase via REST API
const fs = require('fs');
const https = require('https');

const sql = fs.readFileSync('scripts/supabase_refund_tracking.sql', 'utf8');
const envContent = fs.readFileSync('.env.local', 'utf8');

function getEnv(key) {
  const lines = envContent.split('\n');
  for (const line of lines) {
    const trimmed = line.trim();
    if (trimmed.startsWith(key + '=')) {
      return trimmed.substring(key.length + 1).replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '');
    }
  }
  return null;
}

const projectUrl = getEnv('NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL');
const serviceRoleKey = getEnv('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY');

if (!projectUrl || !serviceRoleKey) {
  console.error('ERROR: Missing NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL or SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY in .env.local');
  process.exit(1);
}

const projectRef = projectUrl.replace('https://', '').replace('.supabase.co', '');

console.log(`Connecting to Supabase project: ${projectRef}`);
console.log('Executing SQL migration...\n');

// Use Supabase Management API to execute SQL
const data = JSON.stringify({ query: sql });

const options = {
  hostname: 'api.supabase.com',
  path: '/v1/projects/' + projectRef + '/database/query',
  method: 'POST',
  headers: {
    'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + serviceRoleKey,
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    'Content-Length': Buffer.byteLength(data)
  }
};

const req = https.request(options, (res) => {
  let body = '';
  res.on('data', (chunk) => body += chunk);
  res.on('end', () => {
    if (res.statusCode === 200 || res.statusCode === 201) {
      console.log('✅ SQL migration executed successfully!');
      console.log('Tables created/updated:');
      console.log('  - refund_requests');
      console.log('  - support_tickets (updated with tracking_id, admin_response)');
      process.exit(0);
    } else {
      // Try alternative approach - direct SQL endpoint
      console.log(`Management API returned ${res.statusCode}. Trying direct SQL endpoint...`);
      tryDirectSql(projectUrl, serviceRoleKey, sql);
    }
  });
});

req.on('error', (e) => {
  console.error('Management API failed:', e.message);
  console.log('Trying direct SQL endpoint...');
  tryDirectSql(projectUrl, serviceRoleKey, sql);
});

req.write(data);
req.end();

function tryDirectSql(url, key, query) {
  const urlObj = new URL(url);
  
  const postData = JSON.stringify({ query: query });
  
  const options = {
    hostname: urlObj.hostname,
    path: '/rest/v1/rpc/',
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'apikey': key,
      'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + key,
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'Content-Length': Buffer.byteLength(postData)
    }
  };

  const req2 = https.request(options, (res) => {
    let body = '';
    res.on('data', (chunk) => body += chunk);
    res.on('end', () => {
      if (res.statusCode === 200) {
        console.log('✅ SQL executed via REST API!');
      } else {
        console.log(`REST API returned ${res.statusCode}. Trying raw SQL endpoint...`);
        tryRawSql(url, key, query);
      }
    });
  });

  req2.on('error', () => {
    console.log('REST API failed too. Trying raw SQL endpoint...');
    tryRawSql(url, key, query);
  });

  req2.write(postData);
  req2.end();
}

function tryRawSql(url, key, query) {
  const urlObj = new URL(url);
  
  const options = {
    hostname: urlObj.hostname,
    path: '/sql',
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'apikey': key,
      'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + key,
      'Content-Type': 'text/plain'
    }
  };

  const req3 = https.request(options, (res) => {
    let body = '';
    res.on('data', (chunk) => body += chunk);
    res.on('end', () => {
      if (res.statusCode === 200 || res.statusCode === 201) {
        console.log('✅ SQL executed via /sql endpoint!');
        process.exit(0);
      } else {
        console.log(`\n❌ Could not run SQL automatically (status ${res.statusCode}).`);
        console.log('Response:', body.slice(0, 500));
        console.log('\n⚠️  You need to run the SQL manually in Supabase Dashboard.');
        console.log('   Opening instructions for you...\n');
        printInstructions(projectRef);
        process.exit(1);
      }
    });
  });

  req3.on('error', (e) => {
    console.error('Raw SQL endpoint failed:', e.message);
    console.log('\n⚠️  You need to run the SQL manually in Supabase Dashboard.');
    printInstructions(projectRef);
    process.exit(1);
  });

  req3.write(query);
  req3.end();
}

function printInstructions(ref) {
  console.log('┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐');
  console.log('│                                                     │');
  console.log('│   📋 STEP-BY-STEP: Run SQL in Supabase Dashboard    │');
  console.log('│                                                     │');
  console.log('└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘');
  console.log();
  console.log('1️⃣  Open this link in your browser:');
  console.log(`   https://supabase.com/dashboard/project/${ref}/sql/new`);
  console.log();
  console.log('2️⃣  Copy the entire content of this file:');
  console.log('   📄 scripts/supabase_refund_tracking.sql');
  console.log();
  console.log('3️⃣  Paste it in the SQL Editor on the Supabase page');
  console.log();
  console.log('4️⃣  Click the "Run" button (▶)');
  console.log();
  console.log('5️⃣  You should see "Success. No rows returned" ✅');
  console.log();
  console.log('That is it! Takes only 30 seconds. 🚀');
}



================================================================================
FILE: scripts/split_code.py
================================================================================

import os

# Settings
INCLUDE_EXTS = ('.ts', '.tsx', '.js', '.jsx', '.css', '.json', '.mjs', '.sql', '.py')
SKIP_FILES = {'node_modules', '.next', 'package-lock.json', 'EDUAI_FULL_SOURCE_CODE.txt', 'EDUAI_PORTFOLIO.md'}
SKIP_DIRS = {'node_modules', '.next', '.git', '.vercel'}
CHUNK_SIZE = 85 * 1024 * 1024  # 85MB per chunk (under 100MB limit)

# Collect all files
files_data = []
for root, dirs, files in os.walk('.'):
    dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in SKIP_DIRS]
    for f in sorted(files):
        if f in SKIP_FILES:
            continue
        ext = os.path.splitext(f)[1].lower()
        if ext not in INCLUDE_EXTS:
            continue
        filepath = os.path.join(root, f).replace('\\', '/')
        if filepath.startswith('./'):
            filepath = filepath[2:]
        try:
            with open(os.path.join(root, f), 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as fh:
                content = fh.read()
            files_data.append((filepath, content))
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error reading {filepath}: {e}")

files_data.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
print(f"Total files collected: {len(files_data)}")

# Split into chunks
header_template = "=" * 80 + "\nEduAI Source Code - Part {part} of {total}\n" + "=" * 80 + "\n\n"

chunks = []
current_chunk_lines = []
current_size = 0
current_part = 1

for filepath, content in files_data:
    entry = "=" * 80 + "\nFILE: " + filepath + "\n" + "=" * 80 + "\n\n" + content + "\n\n"
    entry_size = len(entry.encode('utf-8'))

    if current_size + entry_size > CHUNK_SIZE and current_chunk_lines:
        current_chunk_lines.append("END OF PART " + str(current_part) + "\n")
        chunks.append("\n".join(current_chunk_lines))
        current_part += 1
        current_chunk_lines = []
        current_size = 0

    current_chunk_lines.append(entry)
    current_size += entry_size

if current_chunk_lines:
    current_chunk_lines.append("END OF PART " + str(current_part) + "\n")
    chunks.append("\n".join(current_chunk_lines))

total_parts = len(chunks)

# Write chunks
for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
    filename = f"EDUAI_PART{i+1}_of_{total_parts}.txt"
    # Add header with part info
    header = header_template.format(part=i+1, total=total_parts)
    with open(filename, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(header + chunk)
    size_mb = os.path.getsize(filename) / 1024 / 1024
    print(f"  {filename}: {size_mb:.1f} MB")

print(f"\nDone! Created {total_parts} parts.")
print("Upload all parts to Kimi in order for full code review.")

FILE: scripts/supabase_hacker_trap.sql
================================================================================

-- EduAI Hacker Trap: Intrusion Detection System
-- Run this SQL in your Supabase SQL editor

-- 1. Create hacker_intrusions table for tracking security events
-- (Used by the middleware intrusion detection & admin panel)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hacker_intrusions (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  ip_address TEXT NOT NULL,
  path TEXT DEFAULT '/unknown',
  event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  severity TEXT DEFAULT 'low' CHECK (severity IN ('low', 'medium', 'high', 'critical')),
  user_agent TEXT,
  headers JSONB,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hacker_intrusions_ip ON hacker_intrusions(ip_address);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hacker_intrusions_severity ON hacker_intrusions(severity);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hacker_intrusions_created ON hacker_intrusions(created_at DESC);

-- 2. Create support_tickets table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS support_tickets (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_email TEXT,
  subject TEXT DEFAULT 'No Subject',
  message TEXT NOT NULL,
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'open' CHECK (status IN ('open', 'in_progress', 'resolved', 'closed')),
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_support_tickets_status ON support_tickets(status);

-- 3. Create feedbacks table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS feedbacks (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id TEXT DEFAULT 'anonymous',
  rating INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  message TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- 4. Create payments table (for Razorpay transactions)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS payments (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id TEXT,
  order_id TEXT UNIQUE,
  payment_id TEXT,
  amount INTEGER,
  currency TEXT DEFAULT 'INR',
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending',
  plan_name TEXT,
  credits_purchased INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_user ON payments(user_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_status ON payments(status);

-- 5. Create user_history table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_history (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id TEXT,
  tool_name TEXT,
  input JSONB,
  output TEXT,
  credits_used INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_history_user ON user_history(user_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_history_created ON user_history(created_at DESC);

-- 6. Create study_plans table (used by Curriculum Optimizer)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS study_plans (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  syllabus TEXT,
  days TEXT,
  plan JSONB,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- 7. Create anonymous_usage table for free tier tracking
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS anonymous_usage (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  ip_address TEXT,
  tool_name TEXT,
  date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
  count INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
  UNIQUE(ip_address, tool_name, date)
);

-- 8. Enable Row Level Security
ALTER TABLE hacker_intrusions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE support_tickets ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE feedbacks ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE payments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE user_history ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE study_plans ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE anonymous_usage ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- 9. Create policies (allow all access for now — enhance for production)
CREATE POLICY "Allow all on hacker_intrusions" ON hacker_intrusions FOR ALL USING (true) WITH CHECK (true);
CREATE POLICY "Allow all on support_tickets" ON support_tickets FOR ALL USING (true) WITH CHECK (true);
CREATE POLICY "Allow all on feedbacks" ON feedbacks FOR ALL USING (true) WITH CHECK (true);
CREATE POLICY "Allow all on payments" ON payments FOR ALL USING (true) WITH CHECK (true);
CREATE POLICY "Allow all on user_history" ON user_history FOR ALL USING (true) WITH CHECK (true);
CREATE POLICY "Allow all on study_plans" ON study_plans FOR ALL USING (true) WITH CHECK (true);
CREATE POLICY "Allow all on anonymous_usage" ON anonymous_usage FOR ALL USING (true) WITH CHECK (true);

-- 10. Create user_credits table (used by the app)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_credits (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id TEXT UNIQUE,
  credits INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  plan_name TEXT DEFAULT 'Free Trial',
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_credits_user ON user_credits(user_id);

ALTER TABLE user_credits ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- EduAI: Complete Database Schema

-- ============================================================
-- 1. HACKER INTRUSIONS (with Geo-IP & Device Fingerprinting)

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hacker_intrusions (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,

  path TEXT DEFAULT '/unknown',
  event_type TEXT NOT NULL,

  headers JSONB,

);

-- ================================================================
-- ✅ STEP 2: NOW CREATE ALL INDEXES (columns guaranteed to exist)
-- ================================================================
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hacker_intrusions_ip ON hacker_intrusions(ip_address);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hacker_intrusions_severity ON hacker_intrusions(severity);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_status ON payments(status);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_history_user ON user_history(user_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_history_created ON user_history(created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_credits_user ON user_credits(user_id);

-- ================================================================
-- ✅ STEP 3: ROW LEVEL SECURITY
-- ================================================================
ALTER TABLE hacker_intrusions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE support_tickets ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE refund_requests ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

ALTER TABLE payments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE user_history ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE study_plans ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE user_credits ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE anonymous_usage ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- ================================================================
-- ✅ STEP 4: RLS POLICIES (Allow all — secured via API layer)
-- ================================================================
DO $$
BEGIN
  -- Drop existing policies first to avoid conflicts
  DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Allow all on hacker_intrusions" ON hacker_intrusions;
  DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Allow all on support_tickets" ON support_tickets;
  DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Allow all on refund_requests" ON refund_requests;
  DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Allow all on feedbacks" ON feedbacks;
  DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Allow all on payments" ON payments;
  DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Allow all on user_history" ON user_history;
  DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Allow all on study_plans" ON study_plans;
  DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Allow all on user_credits" ON user_credits;
  DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Allow all on anonymous_usage" ON anonymous_usage;
END $$;

CREATE POLICY "Allow all on hacker_intrusions" ON hacker_intrusions FOR ALL USING (true) WITH CHECK (true);
CREATE POLICY "Allow all on support_tickets" ON support_tickets FOR ALL USING (true) WITH CHECK (true);
